Internal article
Local pharmacies
Filling Prescriptions at Local Pharmacies
Filling Prescriptions at Local Pharmacies
Overview
Patients' orders are processed through our partner pharmacies in most cases. We prefer all patients get orders through our partner pharmacies since each local pharmacy has its own set of rules/guidelines, which are often unclear.
That being said, some patients have their RXes filled at local pharmacies for logistical or personal reasons. We are legally obligated to offer this option to patients - but it's not something we actively encourage them to do.
Tadalafil, Anastrozole, Sildenafil, or HCG
If a patient wants to fill their tadalafil, sildenafil, HCG, or anastrozole script at a local pharmacy, it can be arranged. This option would have no additional cost as long as they have an active TRT subscription.
1. If a patient asks for a script to be sent to a local pharmacy
We'll let your provider know that you would like the script submitted to a local pharmacy. Can you please confirm the name and address of that pharmacy, please?
2. If the patient simply provide the location/ address of the pharmacy
Got it, we'll let your provider know and ask them to submit the script to:
3. Create a TRT/HRT Task ticket for the provider indicating that the patient wants the prescription filled locally.
4. Once the RX has been submitted please check 'medications', on Akute to confirm that it appears.
5. Send an update to the patient.
Your provider submitted the script to:
TRT / Enclomphine
1. If patients want their TRT or Enclomphine prescription filled at a local pharmacy, we can set this up for them.
The membership plan that you have bundles in multiple costs including the cost of testosterone medication, labs, doctor visits, taxes, shipping, supplies, etc. Your provider would be happy to prescribe testosterone medication to a local pharmacy, but I just want to make sure you know that it wouldn't lower the price since it's already priced in as mentioned above. If you have any other prescriptions written by your Fountain provider, those medications could be written to your local pharmacy and that could lower your overall price. If you want any prescriptions sent to a local pharmacy, please provide the pharmacy name and phone number.
2. If the patient is comfortable with this option
Okay no problem. We're happy to set this up for you. Please note that you may have to pay a small fee to pick up the prescription if it is submitted to a local pharmacy.
3. If they are on an active membership and had a charge go through
Create a task for the provider who last saw the patient and ask them to submit a script to the pharmacy the patient provided
If they are a new patient who hasn't subscribed yet, sending new TRT patient scripts to a local pharmacy
We can get this set up for you if you're ready. As mentioned, the subscription price remains the same if the prescription is submitted to a local pharmacy. Please confirm that you're okay with us running your subscription charge.
4. If the task has already been addressed, inform the patient that the script was submitted to the pharmacy.
TRT Plans at Local Pharmacies
We typically don't want to fill patients' TRT prescriptions at local pharmacies, barring emergencies.
We currently have three patients who have their orders routinely filled at local pharmacies due to issues with compounded medication.
Patient one
Patient two
Patient three
These patients pay a discounted subscription to account for them paying for medication locally.
Take Note: Please note this is not something that we typically offer to patients.
Sample Task
Calling Local Pharmacies
Sometimes, patients want to know if their order is available for pick up. We'll need to call the pharmacy where the script was sent. Their phone number should generally be available online.
Please ask the pharmacy if the medication is available for pick up.
If it is available for pick up, please let the patient know they can pick it up now.
If it isn't ready, please ask the pharmacy when it will be ready and relay that information to the patient.
To view the submitted script on Akute, go to Medications and click on View Pharmacy to see their contact details.
Frequently Asked Questions (FAQs)
How long will it take for them to process the script?
â€‹Pharmacy: How long will it take for them to process the script?
Generally, pharmacies will have the script processed within an hour or two of the script being submitted. They typically get the orders processed within a week of the order being submitted.
Can the medication be filled at a pharmacy outside of the US?
Pharmacy: Can the medication be filled at a pharmacy outside of the US?
Our providers cannot write prescriptions for pharmacies outside of the US.
Can the TRT be filled at a local pharmacy?
Orders: Can the prescription be sent to a local pharmacy?
The membership plan that you have bundles in multiple costs including the cost of testosterone medication, labs, doctor visits, taxes, shipping, supplies, etc. Your provider would be happy to prescribe testosterone medication to a local pharmacy, but I just want to make sure you know that it wouldn't lower the price since it's already priced in as mentioned above.
If you have any other prescriptions written by your Fountain provider, those medications could be written to your local pharmacy and that could lower your overall price. If you want any prescriptions sent to a local pharmacy, please provide the pharmacy name and phone number.
â€‹
Will I be charged for the order if it's prescribed to a local pharmacy?
Orders: Cost for orders filled at a local pharmacy
The pharmacy may charge you for a small fee to pick up the order. If you have insurance the cost may be covered through your insurance provider.

Customer.io and Automated Messages
Customer.io and Automated Messages
Overview
Customer.io is an automated messaging platform we use to send personalized emails and texts to leads and patients. Although the Customer Service (CS) team does not directly work within this tool, understanding its functions and how to interface with it is important when needed.
Types of Campaigns in Customer.io
Customer.io hosts several automated campaigns, including but not limited to:
Abandoned Cart Flow
For leads who havenâ€™t converted to active patients.
Onboarding Confirmation
Sent after a patient signs up.
Failed Payment Notifications
Alerts patients when a payment does not go through.
Video Visit Follow-Ups
Post-visit check-ins via email/text.
Lab Scheduling Messages
Reminders and instructions related to lab appointments.
Other Miscellaneous Messages
Additional follow-up and transactional notifications.
Visibility in Intercom
Even though these messages originate from Customer.io, they appear in Intercom, and patients may reply to them. Most replies are manageable with standard support responses.
When Manual Intervention is Needed
When a patient cancels their subscription, please create a ticket called 'cancellation', and assign it to the CIO-unsubscribe team.
If a patient had a video visit and continues to receive reminder messages (usually due to the wrong email being used for the visit.)
If a patients labs came back but they are reminded to schedule labs (usually due to the labs not syncing with their chart.)
â€‹
â€‹
â€‹Workflow for Removing a Patient from a Campaign
Identify the Need for Removal
Examples:
Patient canceled but still receives failed payment messages
Patient completed labs, but is getting lab reminders
Create a Support Ticket
Use Intercom to create either:
A CS Ticket, or
A Manual Ticket
Assign the Ticket
Assign the ticket to the "CIO Unsubscribe" team in Intercom.
Specify the Campaign Type
Clearly note which campaign the patient should be removed from. For example:
Failed Payments
Video Visit Follow-Ups
Lab Follow-Ups
Onboarding Messages
Important Notes
You do not need to access customer.io directly - you just need to create the ticket. Most of the CS team should not interact directly with the platform.
After the ticket creation, the CIO-unsubscribe team will take over.
If a patient specifically texts us 'STOP', or 'UNSUBSCRIBE', they are unsubscribed from texts. You do not need to create tickets for these patients.
Please note that the capitalization does not matter for the automatic unsubscribing, but they do need to not add any additional characters (i.e, an exclamation point), to be unsubscribed automatically. If they add them, they will remain subscribed.
Please don't assign conversations to the cio-unsubscribe team. There will not always be someone monitoring that inbox, and conversations can be missed for several hours if a patient conversation is moved over there.

Dosing and Pricing
Dosing and Pricing
Overview:
-Fountain patients pay an ongoing subscription fee which covers all the costs of the service (labs in most states, support, consultation, medication, shipping, and taxes.)
-Outside of HCG, we do not charge more for higher dosing. We make an exception for HCG solely because the cost of the medication is substantially higher than that of any other medication.
-If we need to process an early refill for a patient due to a change in dosing, this would be handled by the medical team through a 'manual ticket.'
â€‹
Common Questions:
-Do you charge more for higher dosing?
Dosing and Pricing:
Your membership includes the cost of any dosage changes to your primary subscription. Subscription plans cover the cost of medication, supplies, provider visits, labs (outside of NY, NJ, MD), shipping, taxes, and 24/7 support. If your dose changes and a new prescription needs to be sent in, it will not be at any additional cost to you. If a dose change is required after your labs, your provider will instruct you on how to implement that with current medications or a new medication will be sent in for you. Please let us know if you have any questions.
-How does dosing factor into my refills?
Dosage Changes and Refills
If a dosage change is needed, our medical team would work on getting your next refill sent out at the appropriate time. You would not pay anything extra for dosage changes to your primary subscription.
â€‹
â€‹
â€‹

General Refill Process and Questions
General Refill Process and Questions
-Most patient refill information is readily available and does not need to go to the refills and clarification team.
-Anything that doesn't make sense (i.e, a refill is not appearing on the sidebar), a special request a patient has (i.e, can the pharmacy deliver to a specific location), should go to the refills and clarification team.
Refill Information
-If patients want to know when their following refills will be processed, please refer to the sidebar on Intercom. You can usually tell when the patient's next refill will begin processing based on this:
â€‹
Refill Process for Pharmacies
-Refills are processed for patients based on two things:
1) An active subscription (that is tied to the medication that will be filled.)
2) An active prescription has been written for the patient with valid refills.
-Depending on the pharmacy used, the process for the refills will be different.
Curexa: Processes the refill using an API call at the intended time. This triggers them to process the refill without any manual intervention.
The Pharmacy Hub: We sent over the Pharmacy Hub a refill list daily based (M-F), based off a retool page. Once the list is sent TPH processes the refill for the patients requested.
Belmar: We process the refill through Belmar's portal based on a ticket our system sends over.
Common questions:
-When will my next refill begin processing?
Macro: Next Refill Dateâ€‹
Hello, ï»¿Your next refill should begin processing on:
Typically, refills are processed within one week by our partner pharmacies. Please let us know if you have any questions!
-Will my refills be filled automatically?
â€‹Orders: Are refills processed automatically?
As long as your subscription is up to date and you have valid refills on file, your refills will be processed automatically. Please let us know if you have any other questions.
-If my dose is adjusted, how do you factor in refills?
Orders: Dose Adjustment Refill
If your dose is adjusted, that can be factored into our system. We would ask our partner pharmacy to ship your next order sooner to account for a change in dose.
-I made my payment on: but I haven't received my last refill. Can you confirm when my next order is due?
Billing: Charge Alignment for Orders
Your charge date is:ï»¿Your next refill date is:ï»¿â€‹ï»¿ï»¿â€‹ï»¿ï»¿â€‹ï»¿Please note that the timing of your payments is not related to when your medication is shipped. The only exception is your very first payment and shipmentâ€”after that, they follow separate schedules.ï»¿Because testosterone is a controlled substance, the pharmacy canâ€™t send your next order until you have about 7â€“10 days left in your current one. If your dose changes, weâ€™ll notify the pharmacy so they can adjust the next shipment accordingly based on your updated dose.ï»¿Your membership fee includes:ï»¿â€‹ï»¿
Video visits
Lab work (in most states)
Shipping and taxes
Customer support
Your primary treatment medication (i.e. testosterone)
We offer payment plans every 4 weeks, 12 weeks, or 48 weeks. Longer plans mean bigger payments each time, but they save you money overall compared to the 4-week plan.ï»¿Regardless of which plan you choose, the medication shipping schedule remains the same.ï»¿Please let us know if you have any questionsâ€”weâ€™re happy to help.â€‹

Medication Mgt
General Medications Workflow
General Medications Workflow
Overview
Fountain patients are prescribed different medications based on their preferences and the programs they are registered with.
Fountain is still a startup, and we are working on providing more treatment options for patients.
This document will be updated as our treatment options expand.
TRT
Patients in our TRT program are prescribed one of the following TRT medications:
TRT Cream
TRT Injections
Enclomphine (oral TRT).
They could be prescribed one (or multiple) of the following medications:
ED meds (sildenafil or tadalafil)
Anastrozole (also referred to as Arimidex or an estrogen blocker)
HCG (Patients may refer to this as a hormone that increases sperm count)
Enclomphine (as an add-on plan.)
Hair loss medication
Testosterone Cypionate
Enclomiphene
ED Meds
TRT Cream
Anastrozole
HCG
Dosing & Pricing
Refills
HRT
Patients in our HRT program are prescribed oral and topical cream options. They could be prescribed one of the following medication options as well:
Desiccated thyroid (Only be available to those who are currently on this subscription. This is not an option for patient's to begin at this time.)
Spironolactone
HRT patients are prescribed a testosterone-estradiol combo cream and progesterone tablets as part of the base subscription.
The cream is typically applied to the inner labia.
The progesterone tablets are taken orally.
We also offer two add-ons: Thyroid and spironolactone.
FAQs
Is the medication you prescribe bioidentical?
HRT: Bioidentical medication
Our partner pharmacy has indicated: "Bioidentical" has various definitions that make this difficult to answer. Short answer, these products supplied are more natural in the sense of hormone replacement therapy.
Do you prescribe TRT cream/oral medication?
General CS: Do you prescribe cream/oral medication?
Our medical team prescribes oral treatment or offers daily cream-based, trans-labial testosterone (by itself or with estrogen), which allows for easy dose adjustments and even, consistent benefits. Please let us know if you have any other questions.
Do you prescribe HRT injections?
General CS: Do you prescribe HRT injections?
Our medical team does not prescribe injections for patients in our HRT program at this time. Our medical team prescribes oral treatment or offers daily cream-based, trans-labial testosterone.
Are you a BHRT or a conventional TRT program?
General CS: Are you a BHRT or a conventional TRT program?
We're a BHRT program.
How do I use the HRT cream?
HRT MED: How do I use the HRT cream
Please apply one click(0.25mL) of the estrogen and testosterone combined cream/powder directly to your inner labia once daily. I have attached a diagram (insert here) of the female genitalia for reference. The Progesterone 200 mg capsule should be taken by mouth once a night, about 1-2 hours before bedtime.
My HRT Cream melted due to heat.
HRT MED: Hot heat melted HRT cream
Please allow T cream to sit at room temperature for a few hours and see if it returns to a stable cream state. If there is any question about the quality of the med, we can always send it again.
What is the base of the cream?
HRT: Cream Base
Our partner pharmacy sent us this link: https://www.belmarpharmasolutions.com/cream-base/, which provides additional information about their cream base. Please let us know if you have any other questions.
What plants are used for your HRT medications?
HRT MED: What plants are used for your HRT medications?
Hi there, BHRT are derived from soy or yams. You may utilize the following links to view the specifications of each of the following HRT options we provide: Estradiol, Progesterone, and Testosterone.ï»¿
https://www.belmarpharmasolutions.com/resources/patient-resources/patient-library/estradiol-e2-compound/ï»¿https://www.belmarpharmasolutions.com/resources/patient-resources/patient-library/progesterone-compound-2/ï»¿https://www.belmarpharmasolutions.com/resources/patient-resources/patient-library/testosterone-compound-2/â€‹ï»¿
GLP
Patients in our GLP program are prescribed one of the following options:
Tirzepatide (Injectable)
Sublingual Semaglutide (Oral)
While some patients may switch between medications, they should only be prescribed one GLP medication at a time. The medications are dispensed by specific pharmacies, depending on the patient's location and the prescribed treatment.
Medication
Pharmacy
Tirzepatide
The Pharmacy Hub (TPH)
Sublingual Semaglutide
Curexa
Semaglutide Injections
Semgalutide injections are compounded and injected subcutaneously.
Pharmacy
Details
The Pharmacy Hub (TPH)
This is our main pharmacy for Tirzepatide. TPH ships to all states except Alabama.
Sublingual Semaglutide
Sublingual Semaglutide is in oral form and an alternative to injections. Medication is taken orally, and placed under the tongue.
This GLP medication is offered by Fountain through Belmar Pharmacy *only*
The shipment includes a bottle and oral syringes (one for each week)
1st shipment - 5-week supply, Refills - 4-week supply
Pharmacy and Prescription Info Sheet
This sheet serves as a reference for identifying which pharmacy dispenses which medication, the types of medications available, the API integrations present, and more.
Pharmacy & Prescription Info - Google Sheets
â€‹
CS is not allowed to provide medical advice or information about medications. Please direct these inquiries to the medical team.
FAQs
What medications do you prescribe?
General CS: GLP medications
Our providers prescribe Tirzepatide(subcutaneous injections) and sublingual semaglutide.
Do you prescribe sublingual semaglutide?
GLP CS: Sublingual Semaglutide Info
We offer sublingual semaglutide and it is a fantastic alternative to the injectable form! By simply placing the medication under your tongue, it bypasses the usual metabolic process and goes straight into your system through the mucous membranes. This not only makes it easier for your body to process, but also eliminates the need for injections. If you have any additional questions, please don't hesitate to let us know. We're here to help!
Do I need to refrigerate the medication?
TPH: GLP Temperature Guidelines
Our partner pharmacy has conducted stability and temperature excursion studies to ensure the medication can be shipped in temperature conditions up to 104Â°F for up to 5 days. If you experience any issues with the package upon receipt, please inform us immediately.
How can I perform the injections?
GLP MED: Injection Video
Hi there, we understand you have some questions regarding how to perform the injections. This video link goes over in detail how to do the injections: https://fountaintrt.wistia.com/medias/4gw9o1yjkz ï»¿If you have any further questions, you may reach out to us or you can schedule a follow up phone call using this link: xxxâ€‹ï»¿
When should I do the injections?
GLP MED: Injections Timing
Hi there, we understand you have questions regarding when you should be doing the injections. Patients can perform the injections anytime of the day; however, we do recommend eating a meal prior to the injection. If you notice you have any side effects, you may want to change the time of day you are doing the injections, for example some patients tolerate the injections better in the evenings after dinner. Let us know if you have any questions or concerns.

Internal CS Processes
Fountain: General Operation Questions
Fountain: General Operation Questions
Overview:
Fountain Vitality Inc. has been operating for around 4 years.
We began during the coronavirus pandemic in 2021 and have experienced rapid growth since 2023. We started 2023 with about 700 active patients. We currently have around 8500 now, and we are continuing to grow rapidly.
We started with one program (TRT for men) and now offer three: GLP-1 weight loss and HRT. We are exploring other programs and options to best serve our patients.
We receive many patients who are curious about our operation and how it works. There are numerous telemedicine companies, but Fountain stands out for its unique approach to patients' health.
â€‹
General Operations and Service Questions
Where are you located?
General CS: Where are you located?
Hello - thanks for reaching out. Fountain Vitality is a telemedicine company, we have team members located throughout the US and abroad.
â€‹Are you all remote?
General CS: Remote Work
Yes, all of our team works remotely. We have team members located throughout the US and abroad.
â€‹Where do I find more information about your services?
General CS: Website
More information is available on our website, fountain.net. Please let us know if you have any questions!
Job Applications
Our customer service team does not post any job applications, but we have an active Indeed profile listing new openings for our medical team. We usually have at least a few openings available.
â€‹â€‹Do you have any job openings?
Job Applications
All job applications are processed through Indeed. Please visit: https://www.indeed.com/cmp/Fountain-Vitality-Inc
Our team is reviewing all applications. If we decide to move forward with your application, we will be in touch with you. Thank you!
I have not heard back after I applied. What is the procedure?
General CS: Impatient Applicant
Our team is reviewing all applications. If we decide to proceed with your application, we will contact you. Thank you!
Do you have any job openings that aren't medical?
General CS: Non-medical job applications
â€‹
If there are any non-medical job postings, they will be posted on our Indeed page: https://www.indeed.com/cmp/Fountain-Vitality-Inc
â€‹
Please let us know if you have any questions.
Provider Questions
Other than Doron Stember (MD), all of our current providers are NPs. They are from a variety of different backgrounds (i.e, FNPs, ENPs, DNPs), but are all equipped to serve our patients' unique needs.
We receive some questions from patients who ask about what our providers are specifically trained for and what their background is. We don't need to be overly specific, but we should strive to provide general information.
â€‹
â€‹
â€‹What kind of providers are part of your practice?
General CS: Provider specialty
â€‹
Our hormone replacement therapy providers are trained Nurse Practitioners with specialized expertise in diagnosing and managing hormonal imbalances. Each practitioner has completed clinical training in treatment protocols and is experienced in providing personalized care for both men and women. Please let us know if you have any further questions.
Affiliate Programs and Third Parties
We cannot offer affiliate deals/partnerships to active patients.
We have an affiliate program for interested individuals or third parties. Each referral would net a $100 payout for them. They cannot be registered patients.
â€‹
Any questions from an affiliate, business, or unregistered lead regarding affiliates can be forwarded to Daniel@fountain.net. We should not communicate anything regarding the process to them beyond relaying the message.
â€‹
If we receive emails from pharmacies or third parties interested in working with us, we should forward their messages to Daniel Raphael.
â€‹
Do you have any affiliate programs?
General CS: Affiliate Programâ€‹ï»¿
â€‹ï»¿We appreciate your support, but we do not have an affiliate program for patients.
Do you offer any partnership programs?
General CS: Partnership programs
Hello, thanks for reaching out. We'll make sure to forward your question to our marketing team. Thank you!

Handling Additional Documents Sent over by Patients
Handling Additional Documents Sent over by Patients
Overview
-Patients sometimes need to share documentation with us, and we want to make sure they can do this in a safe, secure, and seamless way.
-These documents include, but are not limited to:
1. Medical Clearance
2. Prior Prescriptions
3. Prior lab results.
Please note: New patients need new lab results to qualify for treatment. We cannot use existing lab results for qualification. Patients only send these results over so we can have a more comprehensive view of their health history.
Process:
-If a patient indicates they have documentation to send over to us we should send them the macro: Sending docs
Thanks for letting us know! You can send these over to support@fountain.net. Please make sure you email us using the email we have on file for you. Thanks so much!
NOTE: If the patient emails documentation over to us using a different email Intercom will autoclose the message due to a spam filter we have in place.
-If a patient asks if they can fax it over instead, we can send them the macro: Faxing Docs
We can certainly receive faxes, but we do not check for them as frequently. Our fax number is: (202) 998-4218
-If a patient wants to text us over the documentation, that is fine as long as it is legible.
â€‹
Communication:
-Generally, if a patient sends over documentation, it's needed for prescribing purposes. We should create an RN ticket for patients who send over documents just to notify them.
â€‹

HIPAA Workflow
Overview
The Health Insurance Portability and Accountability Act (HIPAA) is a federal law that protects patients' sensitive health information. As a telemedicine company, we must ensure that all patient data is handled securely and confidentially.
Every team member at Fountain is required to complete Basic HIPAA Training through Abyde, the company's designated training platform.
The training consists of informative videos, quizzes, and a certification upon completion. Compliance with this training is essential to maintain the highest standards of patient privacy and data protection.
What We Can Do
âœ… Use secure, HIPAA-compliant platforms for communication (Intercom, Akute message)
Intercom - We prefer text messaging over emails. If a patient registers with an international number and can't receive texts, we can ask if they have an alternative phone number. Our system doesn't support texting international numbers.
âœ… Verify patient identity before discussing any medical or account details
âœ… Ensure that patients register using their own information
âœ… Share PHI only with authorized personnel, such as:
The patient themselves (after verifying identity)
Medical providers involved in the patientâ€™s treatment
Authorized third parties with the patientâ€™s written consent
âœ… Follow the minimum necessary standardâ€”only access or share PHI required to complete the task
âœ… If there is a HIPAA violation/breach please send a message to Lindsay Burden, Joshua Faucett and Daniel Raphael.
What We Canâ€™t Do
âŒ Share PHI with unauthorized individuals, including family members, unless the patient has provided explicit written consent
âŒ Access patient records without a work-related reason
âŒ Discuss or use patient information via non-secure platforms (e.g., Slack, personal email, social media)
When sharing information containing PHI with the internal team in Slack, please use Akute or Intercom links. Slack is not HIPAA-compliant.
Updating Phone Numbers
If a patient needs to update their phone number, please send them the macro: General CS: Update Phone Number
Hello, ï»¿Our team would be happy to update your phone number. Can you please confirm the following: ï»¿Your full name, date of birth, and email on file? We'll need this information to make this change. Thanks!â€‹ï»¿â€‹ï»¿
We need the patient to confirm all of the information before we update their phone number to confirm it's them.
Once they provide this, we should update their phone number on Akute (it should sync with Intercom.)
If the patient cannot provide this information but wants to update their information, please tag Daniel Raphael on Intercom. He will review the registration logs to see if we can match information.

TRT Overview
TRT Overview
TRT Program Goals:
Our TRT program is designed to cater to men with Low T. We accept men of all different ages and backgrounds who are looking to improve their health, libido, and drive.
Background
â€‹
Our TRT program is our oldest program. We started around five years old during the Coronavirus pandemic. We have over 5000 TRT patients, and we are continuing to grow.
â€‹
Registration
Patients in our TRT program register on our website, fountain.net. They will complete an assessment that includes general health questions to determine their eligibility to move forward.
NOTE: Please refer to the active state map to see which states our TRT program is available in.
Medications Ordered
TRT Patients are prescribed one of three primary treatment options:
1. TRT Injections
2. TRT Cream
3. Enclomphine
â€‹
We also offer the following add-on plans
-HCG
-Enclomiphene (as an add-on)
-Anastrozole
-Tadalafil
-Sildenafil
-Desiccated Thyroid
-Semaglutide Injections
-Semaglutide FastSL
-Topical Minoxidil
-Topical Finasteride/Minoxidil
â€‹
Tadalafil and Sildenafil are available as stand-alone medications for patients. All other add-ons (other than Enclomiphene), require patients to be on a TRT subscription to be prescribed.
Pharmacies Used
â€‹
-We partner with several different pharmacies depending on the prescription
-Curexa
-Belmar
-The Pharmacy Hub
â€‹
Subscription Costs
-The subscription costs for HRT and TRT patients are identical. The subscription also covers the same aspects of care, including, but not limited to:
-Labs (outside of NY, NJ, RI)
-Support
-Shipping
-Consultation
-Taxes
-Medication (including dose changes.)

Abbreviation: TRT Evaluation

Can I use my own labs?
Abbreviation: Labs: Can I use my lab results?
Our medical team requests that all patients get a new set of labs. This ensures we have updated values for all the specific tests they'll need to review and often saves time overall. These labs are covered in all states other than New York, New Jersey, and Rhode Island.

Abbreviation: Billing: TRT/HRT pricing plans

You're too expensive!

HRT Overview
HRT Overview
HRT: Program Goals
Our HRT program is designed to cater to women who are pre- or post-menopausal. The program addresses the specific health issues that arise during this stage of life.
Similarities With TRT
Most processes on TRT apply to HRT patients.
They undergo lab tests when they sign up for a membership plan and receive follow-up lab tests. HRT patients established prior to 10/29 undergo lab tests again 6 weeks after they initiate treatment. Patient's who started they subscription on or after 10/29 will have monitoring labs annually.
Differences From TRT
All HRT patients are women. All TRT macros mentioning men do not apply to these patients. We do not prescribe injections to HRT patients at this time. Any macros mentioning injections should not be used with these patients.
The testosterone/estradiol cream we prescribe HRT patient's from Belmar is different than the TRT cream provided by Curexa. The tests ordered for these patients are different from those for TRT patients. Labs ordered can be found here:
Labs ordered
Most TRT providers see HRT patients, but there are a few exceptions.
â€‹Summer Denny is our HRT Team Lead Provider. If there are patient emergencies that are outside of the scope of CS, she's usually the best point of contact.
The primary medication (testosterone/estradiol creams and progesterone), is processed by Belmar Pharmacy at their Wisconsin location. Add-on plans, such as hair loss medication, are processed by Curexa or TPH Pharmacy.
NOTE: We do not currently operate in TN, despite having some patients based in the state. We are looking to open again at some point in the near future!
Starting Treatment
HRT subscriptions operate similarly to TRT subscriptions and cost the same.
Belmar Pharmacy fills most HRT prescriptions.
HRT patients are prescribed progesterone capsules (taken orally), and a testosterone transdermal cream as part of the subscription. Some patients are also prescribed an estradiol transdermal cream.
We do not prescribe injections for HRT patients at this time. We also do not offer progesterone in a cream form.
Plans
One Month
Every 4 weeks
$199
Three Months
Every 12 weeks
$499
Twelve Months
Every 48 weeks
$1799
HRT Videos
We have created multiple videos for patients in our HRT program.
â€‹
The video covering the next steps after payment. You can access the video using the macro: Payment Video: HRT
Hello - here's a link to the video we send out after you start your membership: https://HRT.fountain.net/success
Frequently Asked Questions (FAQS)
General Questions
How do I get started?
Abbreviation: HRT Evaluation
If you're interested in our HRT program please complete the assessment on our website at: https://fountain.net/hrt The assessment will help us understand your needs better, and allow our medical team to provide appropriate recommendations when you speak with them.
What labs do you order?
Abbreviation: HRT: What labs do you order?
Postmenopausal women
H&H (Quest, Getlabs) or CBC (LabCorp) + FSH + TT + E2 + Progesterone at baseline
TT + E2 + Progesterone annually
Perimenopausal women
H&H (Quest, Getlabs) or CBC (LabCorp) + FSH + TT + E2 + Progesterone at baseline
TT + E2 + FSH + Progesterone + FSH + estradiol annually
Can I use my own labs?
Abbreviation: Labs: Can I use my lab results?
Snippet: Our medical team requests that all patients get a new set of labs. This ensures we have updated values for all the specific tests they'll need to review and often saves time overall. These labs are covered in all states other than New York, New Jersey, Maryland, Rhode Island and Massachusetts.
â€‹
What is the price?
Abbreviation: Fountain HRT and TRT Pricing
Fountain HRT/TRT are an all-inclusive membership.
All subscription plans cover the cost of medication, supplies, provider visits, labs (except in NY/NJ/RI), shipping, taxes, and 24/7 support.
Here's a breakdown of our pricing for our TRT Program: https://fountain.net/trt#pricing
ï»¿Here's a breakdown of pricing for our HRT Program: https://fountain.net/hrt#pricing
Is your program a scam?
Abbreviation: General CS: Is your program a scam?
Fountain is over 5 years old, and we have over 8000 active members. All of our providers are U.S-licensed. Our program is LegitScript certified, and our partner pharmacies are US-licensed. For more info, you can check us out at Fountain.net
â€‹
â€‹
Can I go in for labs if I am on my period?
Abbreviation: Labs: Period
Our medical team has indicated that if you are menstruating the day of your lab work, there shouldn't be any issues. Our providers can read the labs and interpret them based on where you are in your cycle at the time the labs are drawn.
â€‹
Are the medications you prescribe bioidentical?
Abbreviation: Bioidentical Hormones - Are Fountain's Products Bioidentical
Our products contain bioidentical hormones that are chemically identical to those your body produces, triggering the same responses. These lab-produced hormones are not human-derived. Non-bioidentical hormones, in contrast, have a chemical structure that is different from your natural hormones.
What pharmacy is the medication processed at?
Abbreviation: HRT Prescriptions
Belmar Pharmacy fills most HRT prescriptions.
The director of your medical team is a specialist in men's health. How does that qualify him to treat women with unique needs?
Abbreviation: HRT: Male Medical Director. We have a number of providers with years of experience treating peri-menopausal and menopausal female patients. While Dr. Doron Stember is one of our co-founders and a leader in the company, he does not treat HRT patients.
Is there an age limit?
Abbreviation: HRT: Age Limit
Currently, we allow patients to register up until the age of 69 if they have not been on HRT treatment. Patients aged 69 or older can proceed with our program if they have previously been on HRT. Please let us know if you have any questions.
â€‹
Are you a BHRT or a conventional TRT program?
Abbreviation: HRT: BHRT Program
â€‹We're a BHRT program.
Do you prescribe TRT cream/oral medication?
Abbreviation: HRT: Options
â€‹
Our medical team prescribes oral progesterone, cream-based testosterone, and cream-based estradiol. This allows for easy dosage adjustments and consistent benefits. Please let us know if you have any other questions.
â€‹
Do you prescribe injections?
Abbreviation: HRT: Injections
Our medical team does not prescribe injections for patients in our HRT program at this time.
You're too expensive!
Abbreviation: HRT Price Objection
" While many programs advertise lower prices, they often charge extra for labs, video appointments, or extra hormone medications. Others may work with insurance, which creates a rigid structure for what can be offered.
A membership plan with Fountain covers everything (labs in most states, video visits, medication, support, consultation, etc) We do this because we believe treatments like testosterone require ongoing video visits and labs for best patient outcomes.
This increases our costs, and we understand we may not be for everyone, but we strive to offer the best program possible. As a result, our prices cannot be discounted further.
â€‹
Here's a complete pricing breakdown for our Fountain HRT subscription options offered at this time: fountain.net/hrt#pricing Please let us know if you have any questions."â€‹ï»¿
5. Can you prescribe just testosterone and lower the price?
Abbreviation: All HRT Meds from Fountain
In order to manage you effectively and assume responsibility for your care, we need to prescribe all hormones and provide those hormones from a trusted pharmacy. We are happy to provide testosterone as part of a comprehensive HRT program that includes estradiol and progesterone. The cost is the same and the greatest benefit will be having one team manage your complete HRT program. I understand if this is not what you are looking for and I wish you the best in your search. The only way to do this correctly is to manage your complete program and provide hormones that we know and trust.

Other Processes
Insurance & FSA/HSA Cards Changes Workflow
Insurance & FSA/HSA Cards Changes Workflow
Overview
Many patients ask if they can use their insurance or HSA (Health Savings Account) or FSA Card (Flexible Spending Account) for our program.
Most patients can use their HSA/FSA card for the program, although it's not guaranteed, depending on the requirements of the specific provider.
Patients also ask if they can use their insurance for our program. We don't directly participate with insurance since insurance providers have specific requirements for coverage that we are unlikely to meet/be able to provide to the patient.
If a patient wants explicitly to try, regardless of whether they want to do this, we cannot stop them from attempting to do so, and we are obligated to provide them with whatever information we can.
Does Fountain Take Insurance?
Fountain Vitality does not participate with insurance.
Most insurance plans do not cover treatment for GLP-1 medications or TRT therapy. Many patients ask if we take insurance, since they would save a lot of money if their insurance plan covered treatment.
HSA/FSA Overview
Many patients want to use their FSA/HSA cards to cover treatment costs.
Many HSA/FSA providers require patients to provide itemized receipts, which we cannot do through our payment processor (Stripe). We operate on a subscription service that bundles multiple charges. We cannot itemize these through Stripe.
We are legally obligated to create itemized receipts for patients who specifically ask for them, though. This is done through a separate system.
Frequently Asked Questions (FAQs)
Do you take insurance? (TRT)
Billing: Insurance
Hello - Fountain does not participate with insurance. We certainly understand the reasons to try to find an insurance-based program. However, it doesn't work for Fountain's model for a few reasons. T therapy is frequently not well covered or covered only after a large deductible is paid each year. Treatment decisions are often directed by insurance companies and instead of the shared decisions of patients and providers. Fountain's billing model is based on an all-inclusive fee that recurs periodically and covers all aspects of service (medication, supplies, shipping costs, taxes, provider visits, labs, and 24/7 direct access to providers and support team via our text messages platform). We are able to give more personalized attention and time to fewer individual patients.
Do you take insurance? (GLP)
GLP General: Insurance
FountainGLP does not accept insurance. Unfortunately, insurance companies are notorious for making it hard or impossible for many to access GLP-1 medications. And without insurance, brand-name medications can cost thousands of dollars. On top of that, we wouldnâ€™t be able to offer an all-inclusive model if we were to accept insurance.
Can I Attempt To Go Through My Insurance?
General CS: If a patient wants to attempt to use their insurance.
Since we don't participate with insurance it may be difficult for your insurer to cover our services. That being said, we can attempt to give you the information you need.
I Read Online That You Participate With Insurance.
General CS: I read online that you participate with insurance.
Thanks for letting us know. Weâ€™re aware of several articles created by third parties indicating that we take insurance. Unfortunately, that is incorrect, and weâ€™re not sure where they got that information from. Insurance companies make it extremely difficult for both patients and prescribers to get any coverage for this condition. We also prefer to not participate with insurance because we can spend more time with fewer patients and give more personalized attention, including always being available to communicate.
Do you take CareCredit ?
Billing: No CareCredit
You can certainly try to use CareCredit, but we are not aware of any patients in our care being able to use it successfully.
â€‹ï»¿
â€‹
Do you take HSA/FSA cards?
Billing: FSA/HSA cards
We can't guarantee that FSA or HSA will work because many individual plans have different requirements, but most patients in our care have successfully used them without any issues.
â€‹ï»¿
Itemized Receipts
-If a patient asks for an itemized receipt, we are legally obligated to provide it. This is different from a regular receipt generated through our payment processor, Stripe. The itemized receipt itemizes each component of care (e.g., labs, support, medication, etc.).
-If a patient asks for one of these, please send them the macro: Billing: Itemized Receipts and create a 'cs ticket', and assign it to the 'itemized receipts team.'
Hi, our team would happily create an itemized receipt for you. It usually just takes us a few days to generate these.
ï»¿â€‹ï»¿
ï»¿Most patients needing information for their HSA/FSA account have been covered through the information we provide. Please note that patients who attempt to get coverage through their insurance have had difficulties due to how our subscription service works.
ï»¿â€‹ï»¿
We'll let you know once it's been sent over.
Letter of Medical Necessity for HSA/FSA Claims
Sometimes patients ask for letters of medical necessity. This is usually for HSA/FSA purposes.
If patients ask for this letter, please create an RN ticket and specify that the patient is asking for a letter of medical necessity. The CS team cannot generate this for a patient.

Intercom Workflow
Intercom Workflow
Overview
Intercom is a tool that Fountain uses to communicate and manage patient interactions.
Centralizing communication: Manage texts, emails, calls, and tickets in one place.
Automating tasks: Some tasks will now be automated with Intercom.
Boosting efficiency: Track conversations, set availability, use macros for consistent responses, and collaborate with notes and mentions.
Intercom Basics
Availability Settings
Searching For Patients
Searching For Past Messages
Conversations
Composition Box
Sending Emails
Creating Notes
Macros
Uploading Attachments
Conversation Events
Add or remove participants.

Intercom Rules And Policies
Closing Tickets And Conversations
When To Close Messages
-Team members should close messages on Intercom when the 'ticket' or conversation has been addressed.
-Please do not snooze conversations/tickets that have already been handled. It just creates redundant work.
Away status
If you need to step away from your computer, please do the following:
Put yourself on away mode
Reassign replies
Use the issue reporting system (unless you are on your break)
If you are working on projects/tasks outside of the standard CS queue, please enable 'away mode' and 'reassign replies.'
Please do NOT put yourself in away mode/reassign replies at inappropriate times. This includes, but is not limited to:
Putting yourself in away mode/reassigning replies a few minutes before your break.
Putting yourself in away mode/reassigning replies a few minutes before your shift ends.
Logging In And Out
Please remember to remove both 'reassign replies' and 'away mode' on the Intercom when you log in.
Please remember to click on 'reassign replies' and enable 'away mode' when you log out or are on breaks.
Automatic away mode
Intercom tracks when you actively have the page open/engage with the tab or window.
Intercom automatically puts you on 'away' mode after 15 minutes without activity.
This shouldn't happen in most cases since messages should come in, but during some slower shifts, no messages may come in within 15 minutes.
To account for this, at the very least, you need to move your mouse every 15 minutes on the tab with Intercom open.
Activity Tracking
We do track the data Intercom provides us about team members, which includes, but is not limited to:
Response time
Active time
When they were last seen
Please be aware that since data is tracked, any abuse or misuse of the system will result in penalties.
Ticket Rules
Tickets should be snoozed using the 1st snooze, 2nd snooze, or 3rd snooze option.
Selecting these options will snooze the ticket for 24 hours and reassign itself to another team member online during that time.
In most cases, please do not snooze a ticket assigned to you through Intercom's snooze button.
When tickets are snoozed using Intercom's default snooze option, they must be manually reassigned.
If you use the custom ticket states we created, the tickets will be reassigned.
As a general rule, we aim to snooze tickets so that they reopen at 12pm EST (noon). That is typically when we have the most team members online.
This is not always possible, obviously (it depends on when the ticket was created).
Tickets and Notes
If the ticket involves a standardized process for CS (i.e., check for lab results, check order status, schedule labs), you don't need to add any notes.
If a ticket involves an infrequent process or is outside of the scope of most tickets, we should add a note to the ticket to give it more context. The team member assigned the ticket may not know what to do otherwise.
Creating Tickets for the Medical Team
When creating a ticket for the RNs, please make sure you use the 'RN ticket', type.
Do not use the HRT tasks or TRT tasks tickets.
When assigning a ticket to a medical provider, please unassign the team, then assign the ticket to the specific provider.
Response Times
The cap for the number of conversations that team members can be assigned is ten. Anything beyond that will be transferred to a different team member or placed in a queue.
Conversations should be prioritized over tickets. Tickets are rarely time-sensitive issues.
Most patient conversations should be addressed within 10 minutes at most.
The goal is to clear your inbox so that new messages come in at a reasonable pace and no backlog is created.
Customer Requests and Best Practices
'Cancel' and 'Start Messages'
If a patient texts us 'cancel', or 'stop' they will be unsubscribed from all texts and block our number.
They need to text us 'start', if they want to resubscribe to messages.
If this was done in error, we need to email them and let them know it looks like they blocked our number. They need to text us 'start', to unblock our number.
Updating Phone Numbers
Occasionally, patients need to update their phone numbers. We need to update their information on Intercom to ensure they can continue receiving texts from us.
Occasionally, patients need to update their phone numbers. We need to update their information on Intercom to ensure they can continue receiving texts from us.
To update their phone number you'll need to go to the patient's Intercom profile.
Hover over the patientâ€™s avatar/icon and click on the box to access their Intercom profile.
Once you reach their profile, please scroll down to 'Qualification' and fill in the line with their phone number. This will update the number in our system.
Please note that you'll also need to update the patient's phone number on Akute as well.
Once you update the patient's phone number, please send them the macro: General: Update Phone Number
Hello from Fountain! We just updated your phone number in our system. Can you confirm that you received this text? Thank you.
Intercom Emails
We receive emails from third parties we work with.
Some of these emails can be closed, but please pay close to the email before closing them.
Please note that this is not a comprehensive list and does not include emails from our partner pharmacies. These are handled in their respective workflows.
DocuSign
We receive emails on Intercom for completed DocuSign documents done using our account.
These are typically used for therapeutic phlebotomy documents or legal purposes.
All of these messages can be closed. These are not something the CS team actively works on, and these notices are redundant.
Quest Payment Emails
We receive emails on Intercom when payments have been scheduled for Quest.
My name (Daniel Raphael), appears on the invoice since the account was created under my name, but I'm not the one handling these bills.
These messages can be closed since no action is required. This is just confirmation of a scheduled payment.
GetLabs - Missing Lab Order Emails
Getlabs is sending us emails for patients who do not have lab orders added to their appointments.
They send this over as an Encrypted email that you may be unable to access.
That said, you only need to pull up the patient's appointment # in the portal and add the lab order to the patient's appointment.
Please make sure you add the lab order to the correct appointment. If there is an issue with the lab order, Getlabs will not complete the appointment.
Affiliate Emails
We occasionally receive emails from potential partners inquiring about a partnership.
You do not need to respond to these. Please assign these conversations to Daniel Raphael on Intercom.
Phishing Emails
We occasionally get phishing emails on Intercom. Please do not click the links in the emails.
Our spam filter should automatically close most of these messages, but if you get assigned one, please close them.
Do not click the link.
Best Practices
Review recent activity. Usually, patients are responding to a recent message we sent out.
Patients message us about a variety of different things through different conversation threads.
When something doesn't make sense to you, or you need clarification on a patient's question or situation, please review the previous message thread with the patient.
While it is annoying that there are so many separate Intercom conversations with patients, we have to do our best to review the most recent threads when things do not make sense. We are working to fix this issue, but it will take some time.
Best practices - Notifications
The notification noises on Intercom are incredibly annoying!
We do need audio enabled for phone calls - so you shouldn't mute your browser, but you can disable the notification noise you'd like.
To do this, please do the following:
Click on settings on the bottom left of your Intercom window.
Locate the 'notifications' option from the settings menu and click on it.
Scroll down to browser notifications
Toggle â€œPlay sound when your tab blinksâ€ (when a message comes in)
You should also be able to customize it so you only receive the notification noise if a message is assigned to you, if that's preferable.
Again - please ensure you have your browser's sound enabled so you can receive calls, but you don't need the notification noise to see a message coming in. You should have Intercom open anyway, so it's redundant. Thanks!

Intercom Phone Calls Workflow.

Intercom Phone Calls
Intercom Phone Calls
All phone calls are made directly through Intercom.
Pick up Procedure: We should pick up all calls regardless of who is calling. Each phone call should last approximately 5 minutes or less.
â€‹
*Please also ensure we swiftly acknowledge and accommodate a patient's request to receive a phone call. The team member to receive the request should place the call immediately unless the patient request a specific team member(provider/Shift Supervisor). If a specific team or time frame is requested you may create a ticket unless it pertains to a matter you can resolve, then please offer to call the patient first. *
Incoming calls are directly routed to team members based on balanced assignment. Please do not reroute a phone call to another member. This is to ensure we do not miss a patient's call.
Identify yourself, our company, and the patient at the beginning of the call.
â€‹
"Hi there, my name is XXX from Fountain. Could you please confirm your name?"
â€‹
If someone indicates they would like a call please tell them to the affect of:
â€œGot it, no problem. A team member will call you shortly.â€
If you're able to quickly address the patient's question or concern while on the call do so.
If unable to resolve patient concern, please advise them you will redirect it to the team that is able to best address the situation. Gather pertinent information while on the phone, then create a ticket for the appropriate team.
MISSED CALLS: If you have a missed call or assigned an abandoned call, please call the patient/lead back. If the patient/lead does not answer, please send a follow-up message.
Inbound Calls
When you receive an inbound call, you will hear a ringing noise from Intercom and a new conversation will be assigned to you on Intercom.
If they are a registered patient it should pull up a new conversation with the patient so we should have their name/dob/etc.
If they are unregistered leads, they will appear on Intercom with an AI-generated name (e.g., Purple Spaceship or Blue Glasses), and we will need to ask them to confirm their name/interested program(TRT,HRT,GLP).
Outbound Calls
If we need to call a patient through Intercom, locate the patient's profile and click the designated Call icon.
If we need to call a third party, click the Create new on Intercom, select phone call, then enter the number for the party you are trying to call.
Phone Call Etiquette and Rules
We always want to appear calm and professional with patients.
Please do the following:
Express empathy for a patient who is upset.
Attempt to resolve the patient's issue in a calm and professional tone.
Cut out as much background noise as possible.
Please avoid the following:
Swearing at a patient on the phone call (regardless of what they may say.)
Speaking in a language other than Englishâ€”unless the patient asks or it is clear, they speak another language.
Reassignment While On Calls
While you are on a call, Intercom automatically marks you as 'away'.
If there's a pressing patient issue in your inbox that needs to be addressed, please reassign the conversation to the team.
FAQs From Leads
Concern
Communication
How do I get started?
You can complete the application on our website, fountain.net. If you have any specific questions, please let us know!
What is your pricing?
For pricing, you may refer to:
https://fountain.net/trt#pricing
https://fountain.net/hrt#pricing
All subscription plans cover the cost of medication, supplies, provider visits, labs (except in NY/NJ/MD), shipping, taxes, and 24/7 support.
I'm already on TRT.
That's no problem. Our providers are accustomed to working with patients who are already undergoing treatment and consider this during the evaluation.
Do you participate with insurance?
We do not participate with insurance.
FAQs From Active Patients
Concern
Communication
When is my next order?
Please refer to the Intercom/Akute sidebar and provide this information to them.
When is my next charge?
Please refer to the Intercom sidebar and provide this information to them.
Can I cancel?
Please refer to the cancellation flow: https://app.intercom.com/a/apps/g17j5kgu/knowledge-hub/all-content?activeContentId=4200885&activeContentType=internal-article&editorMode=view&search=cancellation
Can I speak with a provider?
If you'd like to speak with a member of our medical team, we're happy to send you a link to schedule a visit with them. If you have general medical questions, we can relay messages to our RNs, who would be happy to address them.
For any calls we receive from patients who are at LabCorp and telling us that theyâ€™re unable to find the lab order
Advise them that we can provide the lab order ID number and the name of the ordering provider.
If they still have an issue with finding it, we can offer to fax the lab order instead.
In most cases, the patient will provide you with LabCorpâ€™s fax number.
Other Concerns
Concern
Communication
If you don't know how to answer a patient's question
Please inform them that you'll relay their question to the team, and someone will contact them back. Obtain pertinent details if possible.
If someone indicates they are an affiliate looking to work with us
Please ask them to email dan@fountain.net
If someone indicates they are applying for a position
Please tell them that our applications are available on indeed. There are a large number of applicants, and our team is actively reviewing each one. If we would like to move forward with an interview, we will contact them directly.
If they ask us to relay a message to either Dan, Bill, or Lindsay
Please tell them that the most efficient way to reach them is to email them directly.
It's inefficient for us to slack them messages/use other communication methods that will give them redundant information.

Internal Assignment and Routing Workflow
Internal Assignment and Routing Workflow
Intercom Team Routing Workflow
To ensure efficient handling of patient inquiries and operational issues, use this guide to assign Intercom tickets to the correct teams. Do not assign tickets to individuals in most cases.
1. Customer Support (CS)
Handles:
General patient inquiries
Non-billing-related issues
Do Not Assign:
Billing, medical, refill, or critical concerns
2. Billing
Handles:
All patient billing-related questions and issues
3. Registered Nurses (RNs)
Handles:
Medical issues
Side effects
General treatment questions
Procedures like therapeutic phlebotomy
4. Refills and Clarifications
Handles:
Pharmacy communications
Refill-related issues (e.g., wrong delivery address, delays)
Clarifications from partner pharmacies
5. Shift Supervisor
Handles:
Irate patients (that do not rise to the level of Critical Escalations)
Requests for medical docs (i.e, a PDF copy of lab results).
Any situations that require a patient to use third-party labs
Any issues not clearly covered by existing documentation or workflows
6. Critical Escalations
Handles:
Serious threats or urgent patient safety issues
Examples include:
Patient using threatening language (i.e "I'll sue your company.")
Reports of significant medical side effects
7. CIO Unsubscribe
Handles:
Requests to unsubscribe from communications, or when a patient cancels their subscription.
Important Notes:
Do NOT assign conversations to this team directly
Instead, create a ticket and assign it to the CIO Unsubscribe team
The team is not available 24/7, so any conversations assigned to this team may not be addressed immediately.
8. Itemized Receipts
Handles:
Patient requests itemized receipts
Action:
Create a ticket and assign to Itemized Receipts team
âœ… General Guidelines
Assign only to teams, not individual agents
When unsure, route to the Shift Supervisor team
Always check current documentation before escalating

General lab info
Lab Tests and Expanded Labs
Lab Tests and Expanded Labs
Patients who register for our HRT or TRT program all have standard lab order sets that automatically order for them after registering.
GLP patients typically don't need lab work. They are typically able to be prescribed after the call. A small percentage of patients need lab work due to comorbidities, which are ordered for them manually after their initial video visit by our medical team.
These tests are covered in the evaluation for patients in most states (outside of NY, NJ, MD, MA, and RI.)
â€‹
Tests ordered
The tests ordered for each patient differ based on the program they are in.
TRT Patients
TRT patients have the following tests ordered:
Testosterone
Testosterone Free
Direct
Prostate-Specific Ag
Luteinizing Hormone(LH)
CBC
Platelet
No Differential
Estradiol
Albumin
Sex Horm Binding Glob
Seru
HRT Patients
HRT patients have the following tests ordered:
FSH
CBC
Platelet
No Differential
Testosterone
Progesterone
Estradiol
TSH
GLP Patients
GLP patients have the following tests ordered:
TSH
Lipid Panel
Hemoglobin A1c
Comp. Metabolic Panel
NOTE: GLP patients rarely need lab tests done.
Follow-up Testing
TRT and HRT patients have their first follow-up lab appointment after 6-8 weeks of treatment.
TRT Patients
TRT follow-up labs typically include:
CBC
Platelet
No Differential
Testosterone
Free/Tot Equilib
Estradiol
HRT Patients
HRT follow-up labs typically include:
Testosterone
Progesterone
Estradiol
Thyroid Panel With TSH
Venipuncture
CBC
Platelet
No Differential
Take Note:
Schedule based on first hormone prescription/shipment:
6 weeks: automated lab reminder
8 weeks: after first video visit with labs follow-up
90 days: after 8-week visit
We may resume offering expanded labs
â€‹
â€‹
Overview
We created an expanded labs package for TRT and HRT patients interested in expanded testing.
We do not offer this option to GLP patients. It is worth noting that we have GLP patients who have registered for our HRT or TRT program and are eligible.
Patients in states where we offer client billing see options for expanded testing.
Patients in states like NY, NJ, and MD will not have this option available to them since they need to use their insurance for the bloodwork.
Communication and Process
Most patients chose these options during the evaluation and then went through the same process as any other new sign-up.
If they want to order the tests after they complete the evaluation, we can still offer this to them - but the tests will need to be ordered manually, and we'll need to process a one-time payment for them.
Take Note: We can only use these labs for patients using LabCorp or Getlabs. We cannot order these labs for patients who go to Quest at this time.
1. If they are interested, you can let them know that we can order an expanded lab panel for them.
Labs: Expanded Labs - TRT
Great. We're happy to help you with that. Here's a link to our expanded lab packages: https://blog.fountaintrt.com/fountaintrt-extra-labsets/
Please let us know what tests you would like to order, and we'll get that set up for you.
Once you confirm your preference, please let us know, and we'll run a one-time charge for the panel. Please also confirm that the payment method we have on file ending with: is your preferred payment method. Thank you!
Labs: Expanded Labs - HRT
Great. We're happy to help you with that. Here's a link to our expanded lab packages: https://blog.fountaintrt.com/fountainhrt-extra-labsets/
Please let us know what tests you would like to order, and we'll get that set up for you. 
Once you confirm your preference, please let us know, and we'll run a one-time charge for the panel. Please also confirm that the payment method we have on file ending with: is your preferred payment method. Thank you!
2. Once they confirm their preference, charge their card for the selected panel and create a ticket for the RNs to order the labs.
In the ticket description, specify which panel was ordered.
Also, take note of the lab tests they are interested in, and if they choose to proceed, process the payment through Stripe.
Labs: Expanded Labs - Charging
Got it. Please let us know which tests you'd like to add, and we'll process the charge. We'll ask our medical team to submit a new lab order for you once the charge goes through.
Labs: Expanded Labs - Successfully charged
We successfully charged your payment method for the expanded lab panel. We'll ask our medical team to submit a new lab order for you, and let you know once it's in.
3. Once the charge has been processed, please create an RN ticket. Please indicate what tests the patient requested.
4. Create a ticket for yourself to follow up and check if the RN team has submitted the lab order.
5. Once the ticket reopens, please check to see if the order ID has been forwarded to the patient. If it has, please complete the ticket.
Expanded labs for existing patients
We can offer expanded labs to existing TRT and HRT patients who are interested.
They will need to pay the usual rate for the tests since these tests are not covered by a regular subscription.
We cannot offer discounts since we pay LabCorp directly for the tests, and they charge us a premium for expanded labs.
Refunding Expanded Labs
Sometimes, new sign-ups who chose the expanded labs during the evaluation just want the regular panel. The expanded lab fee is refundable if the patient hasn't undergone the bloodwork.
If they only want the standard panel, please process the refund for the expanded lab work and create a ticket for the RNs to order the standard panel. Please ensure you do this - otherwise, LabCorp will process the expanded panel for them at no cost.
Insurance on Expanded Labs
Some patients ask if they can order expanded labs using their insurance.
Technically, our system can accommodate this through a manual workaround. Still, we should not do it since we don't participate with insurance, which goes against the medical team's standard lab ordering system.
If a patient asks if they can order expanded labs using their insurance, please send them the macro: Expanded Labs: No Insurance
Fountain does not participate with insurance, and due to how our system is structured, we can only order labs if theyâ€™re paid for upfront. Weâ€™re happy to take care of placing the order for you, and once it's processed, there wonâ€™t be any additional fees when you arrive at the lab. If you have any questions, please donâ€™t hesitate to reach out. Thank you!
Other Questions
We cannot order tests outside of the tests listed in the document. This is due to how our backend works.
We cannot order individual tests for patients listed in the document. We cannot decouple the tests from each other due to the way lab ordering works.
We can order these lab tests for patients in states where we do not offer client billing (NY, MD, NJ, MA, RI), and they will not need to pay for the panel. They will need to use their insurance to cover the costs.
We could order the tests for a patient using a third-party provider for the lab work. They will need to pay using their insurance, but we do not need to charge them for this panel.
We want to avoid this since it adds greater complexity to the process, but if a patient is determined, we can offer this.
Panels
We break up the TRT and HRT lab panels since the costs are different.
Each document has its section divided based on the lab test costs. The tests are ordered as part of one package (i.e, critical anti-aging nutrients package)
All panels can be ordered for both types of patients for $199 if they prefer the complete package.

General lab info
Lab Tests and Expanded Labs
Lab Tests and Expanded Labs
Patients who register for our HRT or TRT program all have standard lab order sets that automatically order for them after registering.
GLP patients typically don't need lab work. They are typically able to be prescribed after the call. A small percentage of patients need lab work due to comorbidities, which are ordered for them manually after their initial video visit by our medical team.
These tests are covered in the evaluation for patients in most states (outside of NY, NJ, MD, MA, and RI.)
â€‹
Tests ordered
The tests ordered for each patient differ based on the program they are in.
TRT Patients
TRT patients have the following tests ordered:
Testosterone
Testosterone Free
Direct
Prostate-Specific Ag
Luteinizing Hormone(LH)
CBC
Platelet
No Differential
Estradiol
Albumin
Sex Horm Binding Glob
Seru
HRT Patients
HRT patients have the following tests ordered:
FSH
CBC
Platelet
No Differential
Testosterone
Progesterone
Estradiol
TSH
GLP Patients
GLP patients have the following tests ordered:
TSH
Lipid Panel
Hemoglobin A1c
Comp. Metabolic Panel
NOTE: GLP patients rarely need lab tests done.
Follow-up Testing
TRT and HRT patients have their first follow-up lab appointment after 6-8 weeks of treatment.
TRT Patients
TRT follow-up labs typically include:
CBC
Platelet
No Differential
Testosterone
Free/Tot Equilib
Estradiol
HRT Patients
HRT follow-up labs typically include:
Testosterone
Progesterone
Estradiol
Thyroid Panel With TSH
Venipuncture
CBC
Platelet
No Differential
Take Note:
Schedule based on first hormone prescription/shipment:
6 weeks: automated lab reminder
8 weeks: after first video visit with labs follow-up
90 days: after 8-week visit
We may resume offering expanded labs
â€‹
â€‹
Overview
We created an expanded labs package for TRT and HRT patients interested in expanded testing.
We do not offer this option to GLP patients. It is worth noting that we have GLP patients who have registered for our HRT or TRT program and are eligible.
Patients in states where we offer client billing see options for expanded testing.
Patients in states like NY, NJ, and MD will not have this option available to them since they need to use their insurance for the bloodwork.
Communication and Process
Most patients chose these options during the evaluation and then went through the same process as any other new sign-up.
If they want to order the tests after they complete the evaluation, we can still offer this to them - but the tests will need to be ordered manually, and we'll need to process a one-time payment for them.
Take Note: We can only use these labs for patients using LabCorp or Getlabs. We cannot order these labs for patients who go to Quest at this time.
1. If they are interested, you can let them know that we can order an expanded lab panel for them.
Labs: Expanded Labs - TRT
Great. We're happy to help you with that. Here's a link to our expanded lab packages: https://blog.fountaintrt.com/fountaintrt-extra-labsets/
Please let us know what tests you would like to order, and we'll get that set up for you.
Once you confirm your preference, please let us know, and we'll run a one-time charge for the panel. Please also confirm that the payment method we have on file ending with: is your preferred payment method. Thank you!
Labs: Expanded Labs - HRT
Great. We're happy to help you with that. Here's a link to our expanded lab packages: https://blog.fountaintrt.com/fountainhrt-extra-labsets/
Please let us know what tests you would like to order, and we'll get that set up for you. 
Once you confirm your preference, please let us know, and we'll run a one-time charge for the panel. Please also confirm that the payment method we have on file ending with: is your preferred payment method. Thank you!
2. Once they confirm their preference, charge their card for the selected panel and create a ticket for the RNs to order the labs.
In the ticket description, specify which panel was ordered.
Also, take note of the lab tests they are interested in, and if they choose to proceed, process the payment through Stripe.
Labs: Expanded Labs - Charging
Got it. Please let us know which tests you'd like to add, and we'll process the charge. We'll ask our medical team to submit a new lab order for you once the charge goes through.
Labs: Expanded Labs - Successfully charged
We successfully charged your payment method for the expanded lab panel. We'll ask our medical team to submit a new lab order for you, and let you know once it's in.
3. Once the charge has been processed, please create an RN ticket. Please indicate what tests the patient requested.
4. Create a ticket for yourself to follow up and check if the RN team has submitted the lab order.
5. Once the ticket reopens, please check to see if the order ID has been forwarded to the patient. If it has, please complete the ticket.
Expanded labs for existing patients
We can offer expanded labs to existing TRT and HRT patients who are interested.
They will need to pay the usual rate for the tests since these tests are not covered by a regular subscription.
We cannot offer discounts since we pay LabCorp directly for the tests, and they charge us a premium for expanded labs.
Refunding Expanded Labs
Sometimes, new sign-ups who chose the expanded labs during the evaluation just want the regular panel. The expanded lab fee is refundable if the patient hasn't undergone the bloodwork.
If they only want the standard panel, please process the refund for the expanded lab work and create a ticket for the RNs to order the standard panel. Please ensure you do this - otherwise, LabCorp will process the expanded panel for them at no cost.
Insurance on Expanded Labs
Some patients ask if they can order expanded labs using their insurance.
Technically, our system can accommodate this through a manual workaround. Still, we should not do it since we don't participate with insurance, which goes against the medical team's standard lab ordering system.
If a patient asks if they can order expanded labs using their insurance, please send them the macro: Expanded Labs: No Insurance
Fountain does not participate with insurance, and due to how our system is structured, we can only order labs if theyâ€™re paid for upfront. Weâ€™re happy to take care of placing the order for you, and once it's processed, there wonâ€™t be any additional fees when you arrive at the lab. If you have any questions, please donâ€™t hesitate to reach out. Thank you!
Other Questions
We cannot order tests outside of the tests listed in the document. This is due to how our backend works.
We cannot order individual tests for patients listed in the document. We cannot decouple the tests from each other due to the way lab ordering works.
We can order these lab tests for patients in states where we do not offer client billing (NY, MD, NJ, MA, RI), and they will not need to pay for the panel. They will need to use their insurance to cover the costs.
We could order the tests for a patient using a third-party provider for the lab work. They will need to pay using their insurance, but we do not need to charge them for this panel.
We want to avoid this since it adds greater complexity to the process, but if a patient is determined, we can offer this.

LabCorp
TRT Labcorp Link Workflow
TRT Labcorp Link Workflow
Overview
LabCorp syncs patient results directly through Labcorplink.
Once a patient's report has been processed by LabCorp, we should be able to see it in the portal.
Once we receive a patient's *full report* (not their preliminary results), we should send them a scheduling link to review their scheduling with a provider.
Accessing Labcorp Link
Go to Labcorp Link and sign in using the following login details:
Use your provided log in to access the portal.
Navigation Tutorial Video
Navigation Video
To do so, click the drop-down menu next to Print button and select Download.
Searching for Lab Results
From the main menu, click Results Search.
Enter the Patient Information (First and Last Name, and Date of Birth) and filter the Account Information if necessary, then click Search.
Check the Reported and Collected columns and select the most recent lab result.
Check the Status column to determine whether we have preliminary results available, or the full report. Please wait for the full report to come back before sending them a scheduling link.
Most patients should have their results synced to Akute Health (our EHR system), so we shouldn't need to download their results.
If, for some reason, the report doesn't sync with the chart and we need to copy it over, we can download it through Labcorplink.
Preliminary versus Final Results
Patients are often anxious to get their results as soon as possible. Labcorp may tell them they will receive results within 48 hours, but that doesn't account for all of the results - only preliminary results.
Frequently Asked Questions
They told me the report would come back within 48 hours. Why haven't you received it yet?
Will my results be posted in Labcorp's portal?
