Quest
Quest Diagnostics Workflow
Quest Diagnostics Workflow
Overview
Quest Diagnostics will soon be our primary lab partners, functioning similarly to Labcorp, but often available in areas where Labcorp is not.
We can cover lab fees for TRT, HRT, and GLP patients through Quest, except for those in New York, New Jersey, Maryland, Massachusetts, or Rhode Island. We do not currently patients in non-client billing states to use Quest.
Macro title is 'Quest: Option'
Manual Lab Order
Typically an automated lab order will be available in the patient's chart. However, in select cases, these orders may be out of date or need adjustments. If a manual lab order is needed, you may follow the following process.
After the patient is scheduled, create an Intercom Back-office Ticket with the following information.
Title
Description
Add any relevant information to the ticket.
Ticket Type
RN Ticket
Lab Order Needed
Select which of the following:
Quest (New Patient) for new patients
Quest (Existing Patient) for existing patients.
Once the ticket is created, it will automatically be assigned to an RN in the patient's state.
How to assist a patient with self-scheduling a lab appointment
â€‹
â€‹
-On the right-hand side bar of a ticket/conversation in intercom, you will locate patient actions. Under 'Select an Action', there will be a drop-down menu.
-Please select 'Get the patient's lab scheduling link.' Our system should then provide a personalized link for that patient, which will allow them to schedule at facilities near them. The patient will also have the option to schedule with LabCorp(an additional $37.)
-Copy and paste this link directly into a message to the patient within intercom and send.
Manual Scheduling process (if needed)
*Please send the ticket to the Shift Supervisor's Inbox to handle Manual Scheduling with Quest.*
Go to Quest's appointment site
Quest Appointment
Click on Schedule appointment.
You will be prompted to sign in. Select Continue as guest.
Complete the details for Who's visiting?
Refer to the patient's Akute Demographics and add the Contact information.
Select Yes for mobile phone.
Click Continue.
Select All Other Tests as the Reason for visiting, then click Continue.
Search for the nearest Quest by zip code or city based on the patient's address.
Select the patient's preferred date on the calendar to see the list of nearby Quest locations and their time availability.
Select the patient's preferred time or have the patient choose from the open time slots.
Click Continue.
Select: The person being tested will bring it to the appointment under Insurance.
Click on Review Details.
Review the appointment details, and then click on Confirm.
Faxing Lab Orders
â€‹
Confirming Lab Appointment detail with the patient
â€‹Please send the following macro to the patient.
Labs: Confirmation
â€‹
"Here are the appointment details:
Your lab order will be electronically submitted before your appointment and should be in their system by the time of the appointment.
ï»¿Please note, our typical set of lab work does not require fasting. Our Fountain providers are able to read the numbers under both conditions. If you would prefer to fast, you certainly can but this is not necessary. **However, if you have opted to include our Cardiac Risk Analysis or Vital Metabolic & Energy Factors expanded lab panels you should fast.
Lab orders are submitted under client billing, meaning Quest/LabCorp should charge our account in most states. However, if you are in New York, New Jersey, Rhode Island, Massachusetts, or Maryland, you will need to use your insurance.
If you encounter any issues at Quest/LabCorp (i.e they cannot find the order number), please text us immediately. Our team should be able to assist.
You will receive a link to schedule your video visit with one of our provider's to review your results once we have received your full report. Please reach out if you have any questions!"

Once you log in, click on Results to see recent lab results.
Use the Search Field to find lab results by Patient Name or Requisition Number. You can also filter by particular dates by clicking on Date Range.

That being said, when would be a good time for us to schedule a lab appointment for you?
Note: You can also try calling the specific Quest location the patient was scheduled at (obtain the number by googling the Quest location address).
Quest Bills
Patients should only be billed for blood work that we order if they are in New York, New Jersey, Rhode Island, Massachusetts, or Maryland. Those states have special rules for in-person lab visits that require patients to use their insurance, so this process does not apply to them.
Outside of the states above, orders are submitted under client billing, which means they should be charged to our account, not the patient's.
Occasionally, these patients are charged due to confusion at the office or because they provided their insurance card to the office. This will cause Quest to bill to their insurance rather than billing to the client (us.)
Valid Bills
If a patient indicates they receive a bill and is NOT in New York, New Jersey, Rhode Island, Massachusetts, or Maryland, please send them the Labs: LC/Quest Bill Confirmation macro:
Got it. Can you please send over a screenshot of the bill or invoice number? Our team would be happy to look into it. Lab orders are submitted under client billing in all states other than New York, New Jersey, Rhode Island, Massachusetts and Maryland, meaning they should have charged our account. It's certainly possible they made a mistake - which we should be able to address.
Once the patient sends over the bill, please check to see if we (Fountain) ordered it. You should be able to see the account name and the ordering provider listed on the bill.
If the patient received a bill for the tests we ordered, we'll need to email our Quest representative. Please send them the Quest: Bill Transfer macro:
Thanks for sending that over. We'll ask our Quest rep to get this transferred over to our account. Please be aware that it can take between 30-90 days for the bill to be transferred over successfully to our account. During this time you may receive collection notices from Quest. Please disregard them. You will receive a notice from Quest once the bill has been transferred over.
Please email our Quest reps, and send the Quest: Emailing Quest for a Bill macro:

Referral Process
Referral Process
Overview
We want to encourage patients to refer friends and family to our service! If they refer someone to us, the referee will receive a one-time $100 credit off their subscription fee.
We do not apply the credit to the referrer. Our legal team has advised that we cannot provide them with credit due to medical kickback laws.
Referral Process
If someone asks about the referral process, please use the following macros based on their program:
Macro Name
Message
Promotion: TRT Patient Referral
If you know anyone who might be interested in our service, please ask them to complete the assessment on our website: https://fountaintrt.com
If they qualify for treatment and choose to enroll in our program, we will apply a $100 credit to their account.
They would just need to let us know they were referred when they sign up. Thank you for the support!
Promotion: HRT Patient Referral
If you know anyone who might be interested in our service, please ask them to complete the assessment on our website: https://fountain.net/hrt
If they qualify for treatment and choose to enroll in our program, we will apply a $100 credit to their account.
They would just need to let us know they were referred when they sign up. Thank you for the support!
If a patient indicates that an existing patient referred them, please send the following message:
Promotion: Referral by an existing patient
I understand that you were referred to our program by an existing patient. Please note that you will receive a $100 credit on your account, which will be applied to your next subscription charge. Have a great day.
Add 'Referred by: [existing patient's name]' to the new patient's User notes
Add 'Referred: [new patient's name]' to the existing patient's User notes.
Patients on Multiple Programs
Currently, we offer three different programs:
HRT (for women)
TRT (for men)
GLP (for both)
If patients register for multiple programs, we will give them a $100 credit when they start their second subscription.
We will not be doing this retroactively for patients who are already on both programs, but if a patient indicates they signed up for multiple programs, we should add the credit and send them the following message:
Promotion: Referral registered to multiple programs
Hello from Fountain! We're glad you'll be on multiple programs with us! This is just a heads-up that we added a one-time $100 credit to your account, which will be applied to your next subscription charge. Thanks so much!
Frequently Asked Questions (FAQs)
Is there a link I can send my friends?
Promotion: Is there a referral link?
We do not have a referral link at this time. If you know anyone who might be interested in our service, please ask them to complete the assessment on our website: https://fountain.net.
If they qualify for treatment and choose to enroll in our program, we will apply a $100 credit to their account. They would just need to let us know they were referred when they sign up. Thank you for the support!
Why don't I get a credit?
Promotion: Referred a friend
We appreciate your support! At this time our current policy is to only apply a $100 credit to the referee, not the referrer.
Do you have an affiliate program?
General CS: Affiliate Program
Thanks for your support! At this time, we do not have an affiliate program, although it is something we have actively been discussing. If we end up pushing forward with the program, we'll certainly reach out to you.
Hope you have a great day!
Can you contact my friend who's interested in your program?
Promotion: Can you contact my referral?
They can email us at support@fountaintrt.com or text us at this number and we're happy to help them or address any questions they may have.
My friend is interested in TRT/HRT/GLP. Would they still get a credit even though I am in another program with you?
Promotion: Patient referrals across any program
Yes! We apply a $100 credit to their account, regardless of which program they registered for. They'll just need to let us know they were referred for us to apply the credit.
Do you have an affiliate program?
General CS: Affiliate Program
Thanks for your support! At this time, we do not have an affiliate program, although it is something we have actively been discussing. If we end up pushing forward with the program, we'll certainly reach out to you. Hope you have a great day!

Controlled Substance Refills
Controlled Substance Refills
Overview
TRT medications are controlled substances. To prescribe them, our medical team must conduct a video visit with the patient.
After the visit, the providers typically write RXs with multiple refills for controlled substances. The specific number of refills depends on the controlled substance medication and the state the patient is in.
Enclomiphene, tadalafil, and anastrozole are not controlled substances. Providers do not need a video visit to process refills.
Expired RX/Refill Tickets
The system will generate an expired RX/Refill Ticket 9 days before a controlled substance refill is due.
These tickets are routed to the MAs who will check the PMP, set the default pharmacy (for the prescription), and assign the ticket to the provider if they can write the refill.
Patients receive an automated message to schedule their video visit 30 days before their are due for a refill, so in most cases they should have had the visit and be eligible for a refill.
If they cannot write the refill because they need a visit, the MAS will reassign the ticket to CS. We need to send them reminders to schedule their visit manually.
The ticket, which reopens at noon, will indicate that the patient needs a TRT refill. Send them the macro: VV: Visit required before refill 1.
Hi - you're due for a refill. You'll need to speak with a provider in a quick video visit to process the prescription. Please use this link to schedule your visit:
After sending the macro, click on the ticket and note: â€œMessage patient when ticket reopens.â€
Once the ticket reopens:
Check to see if the patient has scheduled a video visit. If they have, you can close the ticket.
If they havenâ€™t, send them the macro: VV: Visit required before refill 2, then click on "waiting for patient - snoozing for 24 hours."
Hi - this is Fountain. You're overdue for refill. You'll need to speak with a provider in a quick video visit to process the prescription. Please use this link to schedule your visit:
This process will be repeated two more times, with the same cadence. On each attempt, send the corresponding macro:
VV: Visit required before refill 3
Hi - this is Fountain. We're just following up with you again. You're overdue for refill. You'll need to speak with a provider in a quick video visit to process the prescription. Please use this link to schedule your visit:
VV: Visit required before refill 4
Hi - this is Fountain. We're just following up with you again. We've sent you a few texts indicating that you're due for a refill - but that you need to speak a provider to get it processed. Please use this link to schedule your visit:
If, after the fourth attempt, the patient still hasnâ€™t scheduled a visit, please email them using the macro: VV: Visit required before refill 5
Hi - we're just following up with you again. To get your refill processed you'll need to schedule a quick video visit with a provider. Please use this link to schedule the visit:
Once the patient has scheduled their visit, please create a ticket for the provider they are scheduled to see.
We need to do this to ensure that the provider is aware that they need to write a new RX. They will not do it based on the visit alone.
â€‹
â€‹
Refill Messages
Our system sends several messages to patients indicating they need video visits to process their refills.
At this time, this is sent out to patients who need Enclomphine/Tadalafil refills due to a backend issue that we're actively working to resolve.
These messages are triggered thirty days before they are due for the refill to ensure they do not run out of medication earlier than intended.
Labs and Visits
Patients who are due for lab tests might receive their message at a time that aligns with their video visit.
If so, we should schedule their tests as soon as possible to ensure they are able to attend the video visit.
If they ask why they're receiving these messages if we're waiting for their labwork, we can send the macro: Labs: Due for labs - Needs refill
These messages are sent by our system thirty days before a patient is due for their refill. Once the RX is written these messages should be disabled. Given that we're scheduling you for labs prior to the visit you can simply ignore these messages until we get your labs back - the timing should ultimately work out.
If the patient is not due for labs and asks why they need to schedule a visit if they don't have labs to review, please send the macro: General CS: Controlled substance video visit refills
This visit is to get your refill processed. In most cases - the cadence of labs coincides with new RXs and refills. If they do not, though, our system sends this message out thirty days before the refill is due, so you do not run out of medication.
If the patient asks if they really need a video visit to process their refill, please send the macro: Do I really need a video visit to process my refill?
Our medical team has indicated that they need to see patients in a video visit to write a refill for compliance reasons. 
Repeat Messages After the Visit
If a patient continues to receive messages after their visit, itâ€™s likely because the provider didnâ€™t submit the refills during the visit, typically due to an error on their end.
Please check when the provider last saw the patient and if a new RX was submitted after the visit. Please ask the provider to submit a new RX for the intended medication if not.
If you are unsure what the medication is intended for, please review the provider's most recent medical note.
Off-Boarding Patients
We intend to add an off-boarding process/message to this process that will be prompted by the automated messages. We currently need to get one set up.
If patients contact us and indicate they want refunds because they didn't receive their medication despite us sending out these reminders, we will refund them since no order went out.
We will not preemptively refund patients since we are messaging them ahead of time, and they should have enough time to see a provider before they run out of medication.
Internal Communication
It may not always be clear if a patient needs a video visit to speak with a provider based on the date of the last visit/prescription.
If we're not sure if they need a visit, we should create a task for the provider who last saw them.

Refunds Workflow
Refunds Workflow
Overview
Generally, we would prefer not to refund or credit patients. This costs us money and impacts our weekly revenue.
That being said, there are circumstances where we do need to refund patients/credit patients. We do not have a 'no refund' policy and never will.
Our goal is to ensure that patients can easily receive the refund they are owed. At the same time, we don't want to refund people who don't ask for it unnecessarily. If anyone asks for a refund, we can refund them the appropriate amount if eligible.
New Sign-Ups
We can provide a refund if a new sign-up indicates they no longer wish to proceed and request a refund.
We should not refund if they indicate they may reconsider later and don't request a refund. They need to ask for a refund so we can process it.
It does not matter if they have had labs/had a visit; our current policy is to refund any new sign-up assessment fee if they request it.
Take Note:
Please note this only applies to the assessment fee - not fees for expanded labs/Getlabs. We cannot refund them for these services once they've been performed. If the
Patients who initially select an expanded labs panel/Getlabs and reconsider can be refunded - but once the bloodwork has been performed, it is non-refundable.
Active Subscriptions
Patients on longer-term plans are eligible for prorated refunds if they cancel their plan with unused time remaining.
These patients can be refunded for the remainder of their subscription if they request it.
We do not need to do this by default - only if they specifically ask for it (we don't tell patients when we set up their subscription that we will refund the remainder.) If they ask for it, we should process it without hesitation.
We cannot typically refund patients who want to cancel after an order has gone out. Pharmacies cannot reuse medication even if it's sent back.
The exceptions to this are the following:
The patient indicated they wanted to cancel, but we did not process it correctly.
The patient moved, and we did not update their address as intended.
The patient is suddenly experiencing side effects, which make them unable to use the medication. The medical team would handle this, and we may process some type of refund to avoid unnecessary escalation.
Order Issues
Occasionally, there are order issues that cause significant delays and upset patients.
The goal is to resolve the issue to the best of our ability without crediting/refunding the patient. It doesn't do anything for them.
We should ask the pharmacy processing the order to send it out via overnight shipping and expedite it. This addresses the patient's concern, and is often cheaper for us than applying a credit/a partial refund.
There may still be circumstances where we apply a credit/partial refund, but that should not be the standard approach to these situations.
Patients Who Do Not Remember Their Charge Dates
Patients subscribe to an active plan billed on a recurring cycle.
They do not 'authorize' charges. They consent to a subscription plan with recurring charges.
If a patient is unaware of this, we must inform them that they are on a recurring plan. We can send them a screenshot of our message when we set up their plan.
If they want to cancel and no order has been processed, we can process a refund. If the order has been processed, we cannot process a full refund.
If they reach out to cancel and the request is somehow missed or not properly handled, we will process a refund.
â€‹
â€‹
Exceptions
We may process refunds outside of the scope for patients under certain circumstances. For instance, if patients cancel due to side effects and a shift supervisor requests a refund (even if unwarranted), we may process it to avoid further issues.
-If a patient is threatening anything (for valid reasons or not), there may be certain cases where it makes sense to process a refund due to the circumstances. The shift supervisor will handle this. They will make the decision.
â€‹
â€‹

Platform Guides
Retool Workflow
Retool Workflow
About Retool
A platform for building internal tools acts like a user-friendly hub. It connects to various data sources, allowing us to track or cancel patients' shipments, trigger orders, and access Stripe billing links for changing payment methods.
Accessing Patient's Retool Pages
Open Intercom and navigate to the sidebar of the conversation or ticket.
Locate the Patient Chart pinned app. If this app is not visible on the sidebar, you may need to pin it first.
Click on Retool.
We can also access Retool through Akute:
Go to and pull up any patient profile.
Below the patient's info, beside the patient's address, you can see different links:
Open in Stripe
Patient Dashboard
Nearby Walgreens
Nearby UPS
Click on Patient Dashboard.
Retool Sheets and Tools
Retool offers a wide range of features and tools for auditing and automation. You can access a comprehensive list of all active Retool sheets by visiting: https://fountaintrt.retool.com/
Most of these sheets are utilized for internal development or for audit tasks that specific team members work on.
Overview and Features
The Akute ID is combination of numbers and letters found in the patient's Akute link. Here is an example: https://app.akutehealth.com/patient/d314307d-5324-48b6-85dc-4b21781dd5d6/dashboard.
Patient DB ID is the ID of the patient whose OID we pull up.
Order ID (OID) is the number of every order processed for the patient. We use the OID to communicate with pharmacy regarding a patient's order.
Medications New Tab
Shipments Tab
Messages Tab
Other Tab
How The System Triggers API Calls
Our system automatically triggers API calls between 10am-11:30am Central Time. Once our partner pharmacy receives this API call they will work on processing the patient's order. The system needs the following parameters to be to trigger these API calls successfully:
A valid prescription is active for the patient through an API-enabled pharmacy (i.e Curexa)
The patient has valid subscriptions and is not behind on any payments.
Note:
Patient payments and orders may not be aligned depending on the plan they are on or if refills were triggered for the patient outside of the usual cadence.
If the prescription was submitted incorrectly (i.e., a script for a 90-day anastrozole subscription was written as 30 days), the system would still trigger it based on the cadence of refills tied to the prescription.
Triggering API Calls Manually
If we need to trigger an API call for a patient manually, we need to click on the 'shipments' tab on Retool. This is typically required only if an API call is being triggered before the intended system trigger date, or if we are unpausing orders for patients.
From here, you can select the medication that we need to trigger the api call for.
â€‹
After selecting the relevant medication, please select check when ready, and trigger medication refill.
â€‹
Please note: We can only trigger api calls for pharmacies with which we have an API setup (Curexa ). Although other medications will appear for the patient on retool, we are unable to actually trigger the order manually.
Orders And Payments
Based on a patient's RX and subscription status, we can determine whether an API call/order should be sent. You can look under medications on the sidebar on Intercom or Akute, and you should see the patient's active RXs and when they are set to be refilled.
The patient's refills are based on the script/day supply written for each RX. The order should be processed as long as the patient is not delinquent on payments and has active RXs for the intended medications.
Note:
Please note that the information about the patient's active prescriptions, their refill schedules, and their payment status should still be accurate for non-API enabled pharmacies like Belmar, but a team member needs to trigger the refill manually. The system is not able to do it at this time.
Please note that the next fill date may not appear for one of two reasons:
The patient's first order has not been processed, so the system does not know when their next refill will go out.
The RX is out of refills, so the system cannot determine when the next order is due. They should have received an automated message to schedule a video visit to get their refill processed.
Verifying API Calls
We need to make sure all API calls are triggered for the correct medication. Patients may be on multiple different prescriptions, and if you trigger the wrong medication, our system will process it as long as there is a valid script on file.
After triggering an API call you should check Akute just to make sure the API call went through successfully.
API Call Troubleshooting
If a patient has an active prescription/subscription an API call should be generated for them automatically.
Note: Please note that if a patient is currently canceled or has a subscription intended to start at a later date, an API call will not go through.
We should attempt to run the API call if it did not go through as intended. Whether or not the manual API call goes through or not we should create an Intercom ticket and assign it to the technical team.
Skip Conditions
Our system has checks that prevent additional orders going out for patients automatically. We need to manually bypass these checks by using the skip conditions menu.
Note: Please be careful when using these options - since our system will push the API call through without any of the usual checks.
Payments.active-subscription
Allows us to trigger an API call for a patient without an active subscription for that prescription.
Compliance.controlled-substance.po-box
Allows the system to send an API call for a controlled substance to a patient with a PO Box address.
Note: Please note that we do not allow patients to register using PO boxes, since we cannot send controlled substances there. Medications like tadalafil/sildenafil/anastrozole could theoretically be delivered to PO Boxes though.
Compliance.prescription-band.count
Removes the restriction that a patient can only get 1 prescription at a time per band (ie, if they just got T cream, the system blocks a T cyp shipment until 7-14 days later)
Compliance.prescription-band.potential-duplicate
Allows the same prescription to be shipped within 7 days. This is typically used for replacement orders, or early refills.
Pausing Shipments (Refills and Clarifications Only!)
-Patients subscribe to a subscription plan which covers the cost of medication, shipping, consultation, taxes, support, and labs (in most states.) Some patients may have extra medication for the reasons below and want to 'pause their subscription.' We cannot do this:
1) They have been underdosing and accumulating extra medication due to this.
2) They have been traveling and have not used their medication as prescribed.
-CS/Billing will handle the communication and credit for these patients. What you need to do is pause shipments for the patient retool.
-If we need to pause patients' shipments, we should go into retool and look at 'paused medication bands.'
-From here, we select the medication we are pausing.
-Most names are straightforward, but some, like testosterone cream for HRT patients, are odd and display as 'HRT-T.' If you have questions about the medication bands, please reach out to Daniel.
-Once the band is paused, you'll see that the patient's shipments are paused on Intercom/Akute.
â€‹
-This will cause no refill tickets to be generated by the system, nor will any api calls for the paused medication go out.
-What we should do to make sure we get the patient handled correctly is to create a ticket for the intended date the patient should be unpaused for and unsnooze for that date.
-Once the ticket has been reopened, we need to resume shipments and manually create a refill ticket/request so that the order is processed for the patient as intended.
Retool: Other
The 'other', tab on retool has a few options.
1) A link to the patient's unique billing portal.
2) Their Everflow ID - the ID linked to our everflow account (our partner marketing platform)
3) Their home state (we shouldn't need to change it here.)
4) Their Intercom ID.
5) Their Stripe ID
6) Gender: The patient's gender.
â€‹
â€‹

Other
RN Workflow
RN Workflow
Overview
Fountain has registered nurses (RNs) working 7 days a week who can answer general medical questions posed by patients.
RNs can:
Write lab orders and letters of medical necessity.
Address medical questions.
Provide recommendations.
Address patient side effects.
Note: RNs cannot prescribe medication.
Communication Flow
-The RN team addresses both tickets and conversations. Tickets should only be used for specific tasks, like letters of medical necessity or lab orders. Reassigning conversations should handle everything else.
-Most patient questions are tied to dosing, side effects, or their treatment regimen. They do not handle order statuses/shipping issues.
-If we need to reassign a conversation to the RNs, please don't reassign through the typical assignment process. We need to trigger the reusable workflow on Intercom for this.
-To trigger the reusable workflow, please click on 'show shortcuts on Intercom', and select 'trigger reusable workflow.' This will assign the conversation to the RN who is licensed to handle the patient's question.
â€‹
RN Tickets
-The majority of RN tickets should be for lab orders.
If a patient needs a new lab order, we should specify the type in the lab order task:
Labcorp (New Patient): This option is typically generated automatically, so itâ€™s usually unnecessary to select it unless thereâ€™s a technical issue.
Labcorp (Existing Patient): Use this option if an existing patient is due for labs and needs a new lab order â€” whether the previous order has expired, or a new one wasnâ€™t written after the video visit.
Quest (New Patient): Select this option if a new patient will be going to Quest.
Quest (Existing Patient): Select this option if an existing patient will be going to Quest.
Third-party lab (requires provider's signature): Choose this option if a patient is going to a third-party lab for bloodwork (not Quest or Labcorp).
See below examples:
-If a patient requests a letter of medical necessity or another document that the team can produce, please create an RN ticket. Please do not assign conversations to the RNs specific document requests or tasks.
Side Effects
If a TRT patient reports a side effect, please make sure to mark the conversation as 'Priority' on Intercom. To do this, simply click on the three dots next to any ticket or conversation and select 'Mark priority.'
If a conversation is marked as a priority, it will appear at the top of the feed for the person assigned to it.
Side effects include, but are not limited to, the following:
Nipple tenderness
Pain/bruising at the injection site
Emotional changes
Weight gain
Headaches
Ankle swelling
Elevated Blood pressure
Breast enlargement (gynecomastia)
Urinary concerns
Urgent Lab Questions
If a patient is scheduled for labs soon (within 2-3 hours) and has a time-sensitive question, please create a ticket for the RNs and mark it as 'Priority.'
If the visit is happening within the next hour and no one has responded, please use the shared WhatsApp group (Fountain CS/Providers), and relay their question/concern to the medical team.
Order Issues
Sometimes, patients have questions about the order they received or believe it needed to be filled properly.
If the patient indicates they received an order that may be filled improperly, please send them the following message:
Orders: Filled improperly
Got it - we'll get certainly get this addressed. Can you send over a photo of what you received please? We'll forward it to our medical team. Thank you.
Once the patient responds with the photo, please assign the conversation to the RN team.
An RN will review the photo and determine whether it was correctly filled or not.
New Signup: Medical Questions
RNs can address medical questions from new sign-ups. If a new sign-up has a question:
Assign to the RN team.
NOTE: Any patient who registered previously can still ask medical questions, even if they cancelled their membership.
Unregistered Patient: Medical Questions
We should not relay questions from unknown/unregistered patients to the RNs. These patients haven't paid the evaluation fee, which covers:
Labs, support, and medical team access.
We cannot provide free medical advice.
If a macro doesnâ€™t cover their question, send the following message:
Unknown SMS: Medical question
That's a great question! I'm not a medical provider so I can't answer that directly though. With Fountain, you'd speak with a medical provider who would be able to address those questions.
You'd need to complete the evaluation and have a lab report available before you could speak with a provider.
WhatsApp Messages
We use WhatsApp to reach out to the RN if there are lab order issues.
Sometimes patients are at Labcorp and are unable to find an active lab order for them. This could happen for a few reasons, such as:
The patient does not have an active lab order on file
The patient registered with the wrong information (i.e. wrong date of birth, misspelled their name)
The patient is going to Quest but only has a LabCorp order on file.
If a patient contacts us from Labcorp stating there's no valid lab order, the order was placed for the wrong lab, or their information is incorrect, we need to request a new lab order from the RNs.
Take Note: Please note that we will need to update the patient's information on Akute before the RN sends out a new lab order for the patient if that is the case.
Send a message in the WhatsApp group, indicating that the patient is at Labcorp and needs a new lab order.
Once the RN generates the order, theyâ€™ll respond, and we can provide the patient with the updated information.
Therapeutic Phlebotomy (TRT only)
Some patients in our TRT program occasionally do Therapeutic phlebotomy draws.
These draws aim to remove blood from the body and correct some levels that may be elevated due to TRT treatment.
Typically, they draw blood at locations such as the Red Cross or other third-party sites. The RNs handle this process, and CS should not get involved in the vast majority of cases. Conversations should be reassigned to the RNs if there is a discussion about therapeutic phlebotomy.
Costs
Fountain does not cover the cost of the service for patients. We can only cover the cost of lab draws at LabCorp, Quest, and through Getlabs.
The RNs typically advise patients that they can use their insurance to cover the majority of the costs, but may have a small co-pay.
If a patient asks if Fountain can cover the costs of Therapeutic Phlebotomy, we should send them the following message:
Billing: Therapeutic Phlebotomy Cost
The service is not included as part of the subscription. Our medical team is happy to facilitate the process, and address any issues that come up along the way, but we can't cover the cost of the draw.
We'd suggest calling them ahead of time to confirm what the cost would be. Please keep in mind this is different than regular blood draws, which we can and do cover for patients in most states.

Internal CS Processes
Sharing Medical Info Workflow
Sharing Medical Info Workflow
Overview
Patients medical records sync through our EHR system, Akute Health. This includes prescriptions, lab reports, and medical notes.
Patients can access their lab reports through LabCorp's portal independently, but all other records will need to be shared with them by us.
We are legally required to share patient's medical info with them, but we should avoid emailing these records to them for compliance and security reasons.
We should not send lab results or medical notes through Intercom, as they do not have a secure messaging feature. We can send lab orders through Intercom if necessary, although we prefer alternative means.
Akute: Secure Messaging
We can securely share documents with patients through Akute Health. When patients are invited to the portal, they receive only the lab reports by default.
If a patient requests their medical records and has access to Akute, please do the following:
Go to â€˜Conversationsâ€™ on their Akute profile
Click â€˜New Conversationâ€™
Select â€˜Secure Messageâ€™
Click on â€˜Attach Fileâ€™ and the files from the patientâ€™s documents section will load
Select the documents that you want to send to the patient
Click on â€˜Continueâ€™
Click on â€˜Sendâ€™
Akute's Patient Portal and Troubleshooting Tips
Watch this video to know what the portal looks like on the patientâ€™s end
Our EHR system (Akute Health) allows us to invite patients to the patient portal. The portal allows them to see lab results and any other information we share with them.
To invite a patient to the patient:
Click the kebab (â‹®) menu below the patient's name on Akute
Select Invite to Patient Portal
NOTE: This option might be greyed out if they were previously invited to the portal.
When we send a patient an invite to the patient portal they will receive an email from Akute with instructions.
Occasionally, patients have issues accessing the Patient Portal. Patients may indicate they need a new code to access the portal.
We can typically just send them another invite to the portal. This should resolve the issue.
If the option is greyed out, we will need to reach out to Akute. Please tag Paytyn in Akute's Slack channel. They will investigate the issue internally.
Troubleshooting
Sometimes patients have issues accessing the portal.
If we have already invited a patient to the portal and the option is greyed out:
Akute: Invite to Akute's Portal
We invited you to Akute's portal earlier. Did you receive an email from them? The email header should have been: Fountain Wellness - Patient Portal Code.
If the patient indicates that they cannot find the app/locate the email, but we've sent them an invite:
Akute: Download Akute's Portal
It looks like you may have signed up for Akute previously. You can download it via iOS ( https://apps.apple.com/us/app/akute-patient-portal/id1571802525 ) and Android ( https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.akutehealth.patientportal&pli=1 ) and click â€œForgot password" to get back in.
If a patient indicates they received the error 'invalid credentials', we need to confirm their current DOB and name match that on the lab results. If it doesn't, they will not be able to access the portal. We will need to send a new invite to the portal once their information is updated.
â€‹
â€‹
â€‹
If these tips don't address a patient's issue, please reach out to Akute's Slack channel. Please tag Paytyn. Please provide the patient's akute url and indicate the issue they are encountering.
Sharing Lab Results
Akute Health has a patient portal, which displays labs, vitals, and notes in viewable sections. Sometimes, the results do not appear under Akute Labs. It is always advisable to check the Labs section first to confirm whether or not the labs have been successfully added to Akute.
Sometimes, the results don't cross over into the Patient Portal successfully. If this happens, we should send a secure message through Akute called â€˜Lab Results,â€™ and send over the lab results to the patient.
Akute: Sharing Lab Results through Conversations
We sent over your results through Akute Health. If you click on 'messages' in the portal, you should see a recent message called 'lab results.' Please click on that message and you should be able to access the document. If you encounter any issues, please notify us.
Lab orders can be downloaded from Akute Orders. Click the View button on the most recent lab order and Save the PDF file.
Faxing Documents Through Akute
Sometimes, patients need medical information faxed over to a medical provider, or faxed to a Labcorp location. Please tell them:
Akute: Asking for Fax Info
We're happy to fax over your: Can you please provide their fax number?\
Once they confirm the fax number, go to Akute Documents and look for the document that we need to send over.
Click on the document to preview it
Click on the kebab icon (3 dots) located at the top-right section of the document
Choose the Fax option
Once you click on Fax, you will see this screen:
Enter the Recipient Organization/ Name, and their Fax Number
The patient should typically provide this information.
If the patient is at Labcorp and they're having issues finding the order, you can just put in 'Labcorp' in both recipient fields.
Add a Message (e.g. â€œLab order for Daniel Raphaelâ€)
Click Generate
Review the cover page, then click Send Fax at the end of the page
Sending Medical Notes
When a patient requests to see their providerâ€™s medical notes, we should send them through a secured message. Here are the steps to follow:
Click the kebab (â‹®) menu below the patient's name on Akute
Select Print Clinical Profile from the options
When the Chart Export screen appears, click Generate PDF to automatically export all the charts
Download and add the file in the Akute Documents
Send a secured message called â€˜Medical Notes,â€™ and attach the medical notes to the message
Akute: Sharing Medical Notes
We sent over your results through Akute Health. If you click on 'messages' in the portal, you should see a recent message called 'Medical Notes.' Please click on that message, and you should be able to access the document. If you encounter any issues, please notify us.
Sharing A Copy Of Patientâ€˜s Electronic Prescription
Some patients want a copy of their prescription. This is usually for travel, tax, HSA/FSA, or verification purposes. You can find this information by going to Dosespot.
You can access Dosespot by clicking on 'Medications' on a patient's chart and clicking 'Prescribe.'
Once you are in Dosespot, select the name of the ordering provider from the drop-down menu.
Once you select the provider, the screen will display the Active Medications for the patient. Click Actions and select Reprint Prescription.
You can take a screenshot of this prescription and send it through Akute secured message called â€˜Prescription.â€™
Send the following message to the patient:
Akute: Sharing Prescriptions
We sent over a copy of your prescription through Akute Health. If you click on 'messages' in the portal, you should see a recent message called 'Prescription.' Please click on that message, and you should be able to access the document. If you encounter any issues, please notify us.
When a patient requests for a copy of their prescription, we need them to fill out the Prescription Records Release Form. Curexa requires this form to provide the patientâ€™s medication records. Please tell the patient:
We can ask the pharmacy to send over the prescription records. I will send the form for HIPAA compliance to your email. Please fill out the attached form and send it back to support@fountaintrt.com. The pharmacy requires this form to send over your prescription records. Make sure to complete sections 1, 2, and 3, and include your signature at the bottom of the form where it says â€™Signature of the Patient or Legally Authorized Representative.â€™ Thank you.
When sending the request form to the patient:
Confirm the patientâ€™s best email address
Send an email using support@fountaintrt.com
Attach the Prescription Records Release Form
Once we have received the completed release form, please forward the document to Curexa and expect a response within 2-5 days.
Curexa will respond by providing a copy of the prescription records. Please contact the patient and send their medication records either through email or a secured OhMD message. Curexa may also choose to directly email this document to the patient.
Emailing Lab Results
We would prefer to avoid emailing lab results to patients whenever possible. If someone cannot access their results through Akute/encounters other issues, we must securely share them with the patient.
Please send the macro:
Lab Results: Email Requests
Hello,
Our system is designed so that we share lab results through our EHR system, Akute Health, by default. That being said, we do have a secure method of emailing lab results to you. It may just take a while to get the document sent to you. Can you please confirm the best email for us to send the results to? 
Thank you!
Once the patient confirms, please create a ticket and assign it to the Shift Supervisor Inbox. Please DO NOT assign the conversation since it may be unassigned after 15 minutes of inactivity.

Virtu and Sharing Documents Securely
Virtu and Sharing Documents Securely
Overview:
â€‹
-Virtu allows us to maintain HIPAA compliance when handling emails and files containing sensitive information.
-Most documents sent through Virtu are lab results, itemized receipts, or medical notes.
Accessing Virtu:
-To access Virtu, you need to download the browser extension via the welcome email they send out. Please download the extension for Gmail so you can send the documents securely through your browser.
Using Virtu:
-Using Virtu is quite straightforward! Simply enable the slider on your email to send the emails over.
-As long as the slider is enabled, your message will be sent through Virtu.
-Please disable the slider when you are no longer using Virtu. Please make sure you enable it again when you are sending HIPAA docs.
Document audits:
-If you need to remove access to a document, we can do this by going to 'my data' on Virtu.
-To get there, click on 'control center', on the browser extension.
-From this screen please click on 'my data.'
-From here you can see all documents sent Virtu and their status (whether they accessed, viewed/etc.)
-If you need to remove access to a document, please go into the entry on the 'my data' list, and scroll down to 'allowed users', to remove access.
-This will remove the ability for the receiver of the document to access it. This is only necessary if, for some reason, the document went to the wrong person. This allows us to catch the issue and rectify it promptly to avoid any issues.
Best Practices:
-After sending documents to patients, please double-check to make sure you sent the correct document. We can remove access to a document if it was sent incorrectly, so we should double-check.
-After sending an email, please make sure you sent it to the correct email account. You could have made a typo, and the email inadvertently went to the wrong party.
â€‹
â€‹
What the patient sees and troubleshooting:
Some patients may have issues accessing the documents sent through Virtu. While we need to send the documents through Virtu for HIPAA compliance, we can help patients along the way. It's a fairly straightforward process - but here's what patients see on their end.
If a patient needs direct support, we should ask them what they see on their end and instruct them to continue the process and complete the necessary steps to access the PIH doc we send over.
When patients receive an email through Virtu they need to click on 'unlock message.'
â€‹
Once they do this they need to select the email that has been granted access to Virtu (the email that we sent the document over to.) They will then need to sign in to Virtu.
Once that is done, they can see the Virtu email. They just need to click on the attachment to download the document.
Macros and Common Questions:â€‹
Q: Can you send the documents without encryption?
â€‹HIPAA Docs
To meet HIPAA security requirements, we must encrypt protected health information (PHI) and control who can access it. Virtru lets us encrypt emails and files, add access controls (like revoke/expire), and keep an audit trailâ€”so PHI stays protected even after it leaves our inbox.
Q: What service do you use to send the documents over?
Virtu
We use Virtu for sharing PIH securely. It is used for encrypting sensitive data like patient health records.

Itemized Receipt Process
Itemized Receipt Process
Overview:
-Many patients request itemized receipts for their HSA/FSA provider. They need these charges itemized so the charges can be justified and covered.
-We are legally required to provide all patients with this information who request it. We cannot deny these requests, although we cannot necessarily guarantee that they will be covered.
Communication (for CS)
-If a patient wants an itemized receipt, CS will do the following:
â€‹1. Send the macro: Billing: Itemized Receipts
2. Create a 'manual ticket', and name it 'Itemized Receipt.'
3. Close the conversation.
â€‹
â€‹Process for generator:
â€‹-Team members who are creating Itemized Receipts refer to two different documents to generate these receipts:
1. Membership Pricing: https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1TnP3HJX6x0MXqywiSysXoAUJUeyxt5cJkAa3BjieH6s/edit?gid=1276862640#gid=1276862640
2. The Itemized Receipt Template https://docs.google.com/document/d/1tTgO22z8nUXnk7nsZeAeoj61jh-G0lCR7qWsS8valZU/edit?tab=t.45z7o1i7xy6p
-We use the Membership Pricing document to determine the appropriate itemized costs for the patient. Each tab in the document has different pricing. We can also generate new pricing breakdowns via the pricing generator on the membership sheet.
-Once the receipt is generated, a ticket is sent over to the sender. They will send the itemized receipt through Virtu.
Process for the sender:
-Once the receipt is generated, you should receive a ticket in your inbox with the itemized receipt.
-Please make sure the charges on the itemized receipt listed in the ticket match up with the relevant stripe charges. Please also confirm that the patient name, dob, and all other information match properly.
-Once done, please send an email through Virtu with the itemized receipt to the patient and let the patient know.
â€‹Complications and Other Requests:
-Some patients will need rewrites of itemized receipts for various reasons, including, but not limited to:
-Requiring additional information not covered within the process
-Needs a receipt with different formatting
-Needs more specific language for the charges to be justified
-Needs additional information for justification (i.e, medical notes, letters of medical necessity, etc.)
-If you need the provider's NPI number you can check dosespot and review the most recent prescription.
-> If the prescription has not been written, please tag Daniel Raphael on Intercom, and he can provide the NPI number.
-We should accommodate these patients to whatever degree we can, but there is no guarantee that the patients will be able to get the charges covered through their provider.
-If a patient wants an itemized receipt after each charge, they will need to let us know after each charge is run. We cannot manually track this for patients.
Common Questions:â€‹
Q: Can you itemize the membership fees?
A: Itemize Membership Fees
The membership fees include:
-Access to Fountain's 24/7 customer service team
-Access to Fountain's medical team
-Access to Fountain's technical platforms/services. We can certainly itemize these charges for you. Please just give us a few days. Thanks!
Q: Can you provide coverage for insurance?
A: Itemized Receipts and Insurance
Due to the way our subscription service works, it isn't easy to get covered through your insurance. We can certainly attempt to help you get covered and provide you with whatever information you need, but please be aware that we can't guarntee that you'll get covered through your insurance provider.
Q: Can you provide NDC codes for insurance?
A: NDC codes for insurance
Hello, Since the medication is bioidentical hormone therapy that is custom-compounded for each patient rather than manufactured, it does not have an NDC number. Without an NDC number, most insurance plans, unfortunately, will not cover compounded medications, including bioidentical hormones.
â€‹Q: Can you provide CPT codes for insurance?
A: General CS: Itemized Receipt No CPT Codes
Hi there, since Fountain is a subscription-based service, we do not provide procedures or CPT codes because we do not work directly with insurance companies and do not submit claims or billing codes to insurance companies.

TRT & HRT: Starting Subscriptions & Pricing
TRT & HRT: Starting Subscriptions & Pricing
Overview
All Fountain patients are required to have a video visit with a provider before starting treatment.
Fountain patients subscribe to a plan on a recurring billing cycle, regardless of the program in which they are enrolled.
Starting Treatment
â€‹
-If a patient wants to start treatment, the provider will send them a link to begin their subscription after the visit.
-The provider selects this option by selecting 'patient membership' on Intercom and selecting the appropriate options.
-They do this based on the treatment plan prescribed. If you need to review this for whatever reason, you can check the patient's medical notes on Akute.
-If an add-on option needs to be selected, they will click on 'already a paying member', and add it there.
-If a patient reaches out and indicates they want to start treatment, we should send them the macro: Set up Subscription and provide them with the link to begin their plan.
-They will receive a text from our number with a link to start their membership. Please ensure that their medical notes are referenced so they are set up with the correct medication plan.
So glad to hear you're ready to start your subscription! You should be texted a link shortly that will allow you to set up your plan.

Legacy Patient Plans
We have had many patients cancel and then decide they wish to resume treatment.
They may not be aware of our new pricing or still wish to retain our old pricing plans.
We can no longer offer legacy pricing to patients who cancel and then resume treatment, as our current subscription management system does not support this approach. We do not manually set these plans up, so we cannot offer legacy pricing. If patients refuse to move forward due to this change, that's okay.
We continue to offer legacy pricing to existing patients who remain with us, but we cannot make exceptions for patients who return.
Billing: Legacy Pricing
Hello, ï»¿I understand you'd like to resume your subscription on the previous plan you were on. I completely understand that, but it's not a plan we are continuing to offer to patients in our care. You would need to start a new plan with us with the current pricing. â€‹ï»¿â€‹ï»¿

Pricing Communication
Some patients are confused by our pricing communication when starting treatment.
We try to make this as straightforward and clear as possible, but some third parties post misleading information that can also potentially confuse them.
If a patient indicates they saw an ad/website that communicated different pricing, please send them the macro: General CS: I saw a link with different pricing
Once they send it over, please check to see if the link is actually from Fountain or a third-party website. If the link is from a third-party website please send them the macro: Billing: Third-Party Pricing.
â€‹
If they are referring to pricing from a Fountain-branded website (i.e., fountain.net, fountaintrt.com, fountainglp.com), please send the conversation over to the escalations team. It may simply be a misunderstanding regarding our pricing structure.
General CS: I saw a link with different pricing
We're happy to look into this for you. Can you please provide a link to the website where you saw that pricing posted?
Billing: Third-Party Pricing
Thank you for sending that over. It looks like this was posted on: They appear to be a third-party site, and our team did not post this. I'm not sure where they got this information from.
Promotional Codes
Some patients ask if they can use promotional codes to get a discount on their membership.
Our only promotion is a one-time $100 credit for patients using the promo code HOLIDAY100.
If a patient asks if they can use a promo code or send us a promo code, please send them the macro: Promotion: Promo Codes.
If they send us the HOLIDAY100 code, please send them the macro: Promotion: Holiday Referral.
If they send us another code, please send them the macro: Promotion: No Valid Promo Code.
Promotion: Promo Codes
We don't typically use promo codes, although we have been accepting one promo code for a $100 credit off a new subscription for the holidays. If you saw that promo code, could you send that over to us? We can apply that as a one-time credit to your subscription.
Promotion: Holiday Referral
Thanks for sending that code over! We will apply a $100 credit to your first membership payment. Please note that you'll receive the standard payment confirmation message when the subscription is set up - but there will be a $100 credit applied.
Promotion: No Valid Promo Code
Thanks for sending that over. I checked with our team and that's not a promo code we're currently accepting.
Patients Upset With Long Order Processing Times
Some patients are anxious to get started and complain about how long orders take to go out. We tell patients the anticipated shipping times (up to 8 business days, depending on the pharmacy), but some ignore that.
If patients ask why we can't get orders out more quickly, we should send the macro: Orders: Why can't you ship more quickly?.
Orders: Why can't you ship more quickly?
Depending on the partner pharmacy your medication is processed, it may take up to a week for it to go out. We certainly would prefer the pharmacies we work with to get orders out more quickly - and we push them to do so.

If there is a price objection from a new patient or requests for discounts due to competitorâ€™s lower pricing
Promotion: Price Objections or Requests for Discounts Due to Competitor's Lower Pricing
Hello - I'm afraid we're not able to offer negotiated prices at this time. Our subscription fee is all-inclusive (there are no hidden charges.) There are other services that may quote lower prices - but they usually charge for labs/shipping/consultation separate from the price quoted.
If I want to cancel, how do I let you guys know?
Refunds: How to Cancel
You can cancel at any time by texting us before your next billing cycle. There is no contract. You would just need to text before your next charge is run.
Can the prescription be sent to a local pharmacy?
Orders: Can the prescription be sent to a local pharmacy?
The membership plan that you have bundles in multiple costs including the cost of testosterone medication, labs, doctor visits, taxes, shipping, supplies, etc. Your provider would be happy to prescribe testosterone medication to a local pharmacy, but I just want to make sure you know that it wouldn't lower the price since it's already priced in as mentioned above. If you have any other prescriptions written by your Fountain provider, those medications could be written to your local pharmacy and that could lower your overall price. If you want any prescriptions sent to a local pharmacy, please provide the pharmacy name and phone number.
Can you ship medication to X country?
Orders: Can you ship medication to X country?
Our partner pharmacies are unable to ship outside of US territory.
Can the pharmacy deliver to PO boxes?
Orders: Can you deliver to PO boxes?
Our current setup with our pharmacy partners prevents them from sending orders to PO boxes or lock boxes.
How long does it take for orders to go out?
Orders: How long does it take for orders to go out?
After your payment is processed, your shipment should go out within a week. You will be texted a tracking link when your order goes out.
I was told that I didn't need to start the process over again. Why do I need to schedule a visit with a provider?
Start Subscription: Patient wants to resume treatment
You would need to speak with a provider since you'll need a new prescription. They need to see you in a video visit to set this up for you.
Do you have any discounts for returning patients?
Promotion: No Discount
We do not currently offer any discounts.
Do I need new labs before being prescribed?
Labs: New Labs Prior to Being Prescribed
When you speak with a provider, they'll go through the process of restarting treatment with you. If necessary, we'll order new labs before restarting treatment.
Am I going to be charged anything for the visit?
Start Subscription: Resume treatment - VV
Since you already registered with us, we wouldn't charge you anything for the visit. We'd only charge you if you resumed your subscription.
If I cancel partway through the subscription what kind of refund would I receive?
Refunds: Terms for Longer Term Plans
If you are on a longer-term plan with us you would be refunded the remainder of the subscription. For instance, if you were on a 12-week plan and canceled 10 weeks into the plan we would process a refund equivalent to two weeks.
Do you have any discounts for returning patients?
Promotion: No Discount
We do not currently offer any discounts.
Do I need new labs before being prescribed?
Labs: New Labs Prior to Being Prescribed
When you speak with a provider, they'll go through the process of restarting treatment with you. If necessary we'll order new labs before restarting treatment.
Are the plans billed weekly?
Billing: Can charges be billed weekly?
All plans are billed upfront and recur on a subscription cycle.
Does the plan cover Enclomphine, TRT cream, and injections?
General CS: What medications are prescribed in the TRT plan?
The cost of the plan is for either TRT cream, Enclomphine, or Injections. The plan is billed upfront and recurs on a subscription cycle.
Can I switch plans later on?
Switch plans
Yes, you can switch your membership plan anytime before your subscription renews.
If we cancel a patient's subscription who has remaining time in their plan
Billing: Cancelling a termed plan + processing a refund
We canceled your subscription and processed a refund of: to account for the unused duration of your subscription. Take care, and please text us if you want to resume treatment with us later. You won't need to complete the online evaluation again.
Do you prescribe desiccated thyroid medication?
General CS: Do you prescribe desiccated thyroid medication?
Yes! Our providers can prescribe that for you if you are on an active subscription. The plan would be billed at $175 every 12 weeks.
Do you prescribe spironolactone?
General CS: Do you prescribe spironolactone?
Yes! Our providers can prescribe that for you if you are on an active HRT subscription. The plan would be billed at $25 every 12 weeks.
Can I split up payments?
Billing: No Split Payments
We cannot split payments up due to the way our subscription system works.
Can I use my HSA/FSA card?
Billing: FSA/HSA cards
We are happy to provide any information that may help with FSA/HSA reimbursement. Many of our patients have successfully received their fees reimbursed, but we cannot guarantee that FSA/HSA will cover our services. Please let us know if that works for you.
Can you remind me of the pricing plans for your TRT program?
Pricing: TRT subscription options
Here's the pricing for our TRT Programs:

Standard Plan: billed every 12 weeks at $499.
Pro Plan: billed every 48 weeks at $1799.
The subscription covers support, labs (outside of NY, NJ and MD) shipping and consultation. Please note that all plans are paid upfront and recur on a subscription cycle.

Subscription fees for TRT/HRT programs

12-month plan, 48 wk, $1799

Stripe
Stripe and Billing Workflow
Stripe and Billing Workflow
Overview
Our payment processor. All payments are processed through Stripe, and go through their system. Our dedicated billing team handles all Stripe tasks and processes.
Stripe allows us to do the following:
Set up and modify subscriptions
Provide patients with billing information
Process credits and refunds
Access internal reporting tools
Accessing Stripe Profiles
To access a patient's Stripe profile, go to their Intercom profile, navigate the sidebar, and select Stripe under the Patient Chart section.
General Tasks and Questions
Everyone on the CS team can do the following, even if they do not have access to Stripe. This information is on the sidebar on Intercom under: Patient Payments
Inform the patient of their next charge date.
Provide them with the amount of their subscriptions charged.
Provide them with information about prior payments.
Provide them with a link to change their payment information.
Any other tasks requiring Stripe access need to be handled by our billing team.
Billing Tasks
Team members on the billing team must handle the following tasks
Canceling subscriptions
Adjusting stripe plans
Processing credit or refunds
Sending receipts
Subscriptions
Patients subscribe to recurring subscriptions.
We do not send invoices to patients before their charges are processed; these charges are run automatically through our system.
Patients are not automatically notified of their billing date in advance. Their charges are run automatically through the subscription system.
They do not receive receipts for each transaction automatically, except in cases of refunds. We can send receipts over to patients on an as-needed basis.
Adding Subscriptions (OLD PROCESS!)
Canceling Subscriptions
1. Click the X button to the right of the subscription
2. Choose when to end the subscription: Immediately
Take Note: Refund should be set to No refund by default.
3. Click Cancel Subscription after all settings are finalized
Take Note: By default, cancellations take effect immediately. As soon as the subscription cancels, no further invoices are generated for it.
Stripe Security
Once a payment method has been added to Stripe, we can only see the last four digits of the payment method for security purposes.
If a patient asks if we give them the CVC code or the whole card number on file, we can send them the macro: Billing: Stripe Card Security
â€‹
Billing: Stripe Card Security
Our payment processor, Stripe, only provides us with the last four digits of payment methods for security purposes. We can't see the full card number, or CVC code on the card. If you'd like to change the card on file, we can send you a link to update your payment information.
Adjusting Charge Dates (For Shift Supervisor Only)
*If a patient request to have their charge date adjusted, please send ticket to the shift supervisor team for further review. We should not promise this request can be accommodated.*
â€‹
-We will no longer pause subscriptions or add credits when adjusting charge dates, as this can disrupt our metrics and cause issues, especially for patients with multiple subscriptions.
-Our current policy for moving charge dates is that we can move their charge date back by up to a week as needed, other than for patients who have failed payments. We can reset the charge dates for those patients if necessary to avoid unnecessary escalation.
-If there are operational or technical issues that have caused problems, we may make exceptions and move their charge date back further.
â€‹
Our process for this is as follows:
If a patient wants to move their charge date to a later date than their original charge date
If a patient wants to move their charge date to an earlier date
Update the current subscription.
â€‹
Reset the billing date.
â€‹
Schedule the update.
â€‹
Switching Subscriptions
Patients are not locked into subscription plans and can adjust them anytime. The easiest option is for their subscription plan to change at the end of their current subscription.
To do this, simply start a new subscription for them when their current plan renews and cancel their existing subscription.
The best way to do this is to enter the patient's stripe profile. Click on the active subscription and set an end date for the current subscription.
â€‹
After setting this up we should start a new subscription that will activate on the day the current subscription cancels.
Take Note: Patients must be on an active subscription to process refills. We cannot pause a subscription or set it to start on a future date if they do not have an active one.
If a patient wants to change their plan midway through their subscription, we need to determine how far they are into their plan, credit them for the remainder, and charge them for the new plan. We can compute the credit amount by using the Proration Calculator.
Prorate refund/credit steps differ based on the plan:
If a patient wants to cancel, we DO NOT need to process refunds. We only need to process prorated refunds if they specifically ask for one.
For patients on the monthly plan, no credit will be issued if theyâ€™ve already received their medication.
Patients on 3-month or 12-month plans are eligible for a credit if they wish to switch to a different plan.
For patients on 3-month and 12-month plans, prorated credits will be issued based on the amount of medication theyâ€™ve received.
To calculate the refund amount, weâ€™ll need the medication dispensed and the weeks paid for:
Medication Dispensed
Access the patient's Akute profile.
Navigate to the "Medications" section.
Count the number of supplies indicated in the medication entries.
ABS/Curexa - 10 weeks (T Cyp)
Belmar Enc - 28 days (for one-month plans), or 84 days for 3, 6, or 12 month plans.
GLP (the weeks should be indicated in every prescription in Akute)
TPH Tirzepatide - 4 weeks
Curexa Sublingual - 5 weeks for initial rx then 4 weeks for subsequent prescriptions
Weeks Paid For
Review the payment details in the patient's Stripe profile.
The payment amount will indicate the plan duration
(e.g., $699 for 3 months, $2388 for 12 months).
Calculate the total amount of all payments, excluding any fees tied to the evaluation (expanded labs)
Sample computation
Daniel is currently on the 3-month plan but wants to switch to the 12-month plan. He paid $699 and has already received a 1-month supply of injections. Since we've already provided 1 month's worth of medication, we'll credit him for the prorated subscription cost for one month. To calculate this, we will enter the following into the calculator:
$699 as the amount the patient paid
4 weeks for the medication dispensed
12 weeks for the total weeks paid
Even if the patient is only two weeks into their subscription, we'll still credit them for a whole month, as the credit is based on the medication supply they've received, not on how far they are into their billing cycle.
After determining the credit amount, you can apply it to the patientâ€™s credit balance.
â€‹
Then, update the subscription and change the Stripe product.
â€‹
Remember that if you switch the Stripe product to one with a different billing cycle, the patient will be charged immediately when you update the subscription.
The added credit will be applied and deducted once the subscription is updated.
After computing, send the patient the macro: Subscription Plan Update & Credit Adjustment Confirmation
Splitting up Subscriptions
Sometimes, we need to separate patient subscriptions due to order delays or because different pharmacies are handling the medications. Stripeâ€™s bundled subscription feature only works when all medicines are from the same pharmacy.
For example, if a Stripe subscription includes both anastrozole and enclomiphene, neither will be processed because they come from different pharmacies. In this case, we need to split the subscription. To do this:
Remove one of the medications from the current plan (it doesnâ€™t matter which). Simply click the three dots next to the plan and select Remove product.
Once the product is removed, weâ€™ll need to set up a new, separate subscription. Make sure the renewal or start date matches the original planâ€™s setup.
Adding Credit or Balance
On the patient's profile overview tab, click 'Adjust Balance,' which is adjacent to the Invoice Credit Balance header.
Select the appropriate adjustment type (Credit/Debit.)
Input the amount to be credited/debited.
Click Apply to credit balance to complete.
Handling Payments

Enter the Amount.
Add a Description for the transaction.
Click Create Payment.
Stripe reloads and shows the payment made on the account which should appear as â€œSucceeded.â€
Adding/Changing Payment Method

You may need to pin the app if it does not appear on the sidebar.
Choose Get Stripe Billing Portal Link.
Patients who updated their payment method to 'Link' receive text and email verifications. They may ask us if the email the received is legitimate, if so, send the patient the macro: Billing: Verification Email Link
Billing: Verification Email Link
Hey - yes, this is a legitimate email from Link. Link sends a six-digit verification code via SMS to your mobile phone number or email address to confirm that it is indeed you who is making the purchase. Your next charge/refill date for your ___ subscription is ___.
Moving Patients Charge Dates
Sometimes patients have failed payments showing as â€œFailedâ€ or â€œBlockedâ€ under Payments. When this occurs, they receive a text from our number and an email from support@fountaintrt.com. Three messages are sent out as reminders.
Stripe attempts to automatically re-run the payment within 3 days of the first failed payment. This is an ongoing process, and Stripe will continually attempt to rebill the patient until the charge goes through.
Sometimes patients need to change their charge date so we need to void failed payments and adjust their charge date.
Step 1: Void failed payments
Under Invoices, select the invoice that shows â€œRetryingâ€ or â€œFailedâ€.
â€‹
Click More and select Change invoice status option.
â€‹
Select Void.
â€‹
Click Update status.
Take Note: You can only void invoices that have the open or the uncollectible status.
Step 2: Create a new subscription
Create a new subscription of the same plan and set the intended charge date.
Step 3: Cancel the existing subscription
We want to avoid artificially deflating our cancellation metrics by canceling patients without an active subscription.
Refer to the cancelling subscription section of this workflow for reference.
Step 4: Message the patient
No problem. We'll move your next charge date to: Have a great day!

Refunding To A Different Card
Occasionally, patients request that a charge we've already processed be moved to a different card. We can accommodate this, but we first need to run the charge on the patient's new card.
To assist the patient, you can send them the macro: Refunding to another card.
Once the patient agrees, follow this process:
Run the charge on the new card before issuing the refund on the original payment method.
Once the patient confirms and has added or verified the new payment method, set it as the default in Stripe.
After setting the default, click the "+" button in the payment section.
Enter the amount the patient paid in the "Amount" field.
Add a note in the description: "Transferring payment to new card."
Once the payment is processed, issue the refund on the original card.
Finally, please let the patient know that the payment went through successfully.
We ran the charge successfully on your new card, and processed the refund on your old card. Please note the refund typically takes 3-5 business days to go through. Hope you have a great day.
Refunding To A Different Card: After A Refund Had Been Applied
After processing a refund for a patient, we cannot process a new refund on another card, as the money has already been deducted from our account.
If the patient indicates they need the refund processed to another card, please send them the macro: Refunds: Refund another card after the refund has been applied.
If they indicate that they're unable to process the refund through their bank with the provided information, we will need to contact Stripe support.
Please reach out to Stripe Support and indicate that the patient to whom we applied the refund closed their card and needs more information to get it processed through their bank.
Once we get this information from Stripe (usually refund transaction codes), please send the patient the macro: Refunds: Refund Codes
Refunds: Refund another card after the refund has been applied

That being said, our payment processor sent you a receipt confirming the refund. You should be able to call your bank with the information provided and they should be able to process the refund to another card/account. If you need additional information, we can contact our payment processor.

Sometimes, patients have billing questions that are not covered within the scope of our billing processes. In these situations, we need to reach out to Stripe support.
