Next Charge
If a patient asks when their next charge date is, please check on the sidebar on Intercom.
You will see previous payments made by the patient, as well as the following charge dates for their active subscriptions.
Take Note: Please note that charges are not necessarily linked to patient shipments.
Timing of Charges
We do not know the exact time at which a charge will be run. All we know is that the charge will run within the day.
We can manually charge a patient's card if they want to be charged outside of this schedule (if needed), but the system handles the specific timing.
If a patient explicitly wants a charge to be run at a specific time on the charge date, please send them the macro: Billing: Charge Timing
If they want us to run it, please create a ticket on Intercom to run the patient's card at the specific time they provided.
Please let them know if it goes through.
If that doesn't work for them, we may need to adjust their charge date.
Billing: Charge Timing
Our system runs the charge on the charge date, but we don't know the exact timing of when it will be run.
If you'd like, we can run the charge at a specific time ourselves if the system hasn't processed it yet.
Failed Payments and Voiding Invoices
Patients whose subscription charge does not go through receive an automated message indicating their payment was not successful.
Our system sends out three of these messages encouraging them to pay for the past-due invoice. Once the charge clears, these messages will cease to go out.
â€‹
â€‹ If a patient has a failed payment but wants to cancel their subscription, we will need to void the invoice, cancel the subscription, and create a CS ticket. Please send the ticket to the cio-unsubscribe team on Intercom.
To void the failed payment invoice, please do the following:
1. Click into a failed payment invoice.
2. Click on 'change invoice status.'
3. Click on 'void.'
NOTE: There may be multiple past due invoices that need to be voided if the patient is far behind on payments.
â€‹
â€‹Failed Payments and Billing Cycles
When patients have failed payments, their billing cycle does not account for this.
For example, if a patient is on a four-week plan and has a failed payment for a week, their next charge will remain three weeks out.
If a patient asks why they were charged early, and it was due to a failed payment, we can send them the macro: Failed Payment - Billing Cycle did not Reset.
Hi there - it looks like there was a failed payment in the system from: The charge was reattempted, which is why it looks like you may have been charged early. Our billing system does not reset charge dates based on failed subscription payments. Please let us know if you have any questions.
If the patient is upset with this arrangement, we can still adjust their charge regardless to avoid unnecessary escalation. You can send them the macro: Failed Payment Charge Date Change.
This is currently our setup with our payment processor. That said, we can adjust your charge date to correspond directly with your next payment. We will get that set up for you shortly. 
Billing and Shipments
â€‹
-When patients start their subscription, their charges are aligned with orders; however, shipments often do not align with their billing after that. -Shipments are based on an active prescription being on file for a patient, along with their active subscription. -Many patients assume that since a charge has just been run for them, a new order will be processed immediately.
-If a patient indicates they were just charged, and their shipments do not match their charge date, please send them the macro: Billing: Charge Alignment for Orders
â€‹
Your charge date is: Your next refill date is:
Please note: the timing of your payments is not connected to when your medication is shipped. The only exception is your very first payment and shipmentâ€”after that, they follow separate schedules. Because testosterone is a controlled substance, the pharmacy canâ€™t send your next vial until you have about 7â€“10 days left in your current one. If your dose changes during a vial, weâ€™ll notify the pharmacy so they can adjust the next shipment accordingly based on your updated dose. Your membership fee includes:
Video visits
Lab work (in most states)
Shipping and taxes
Customer support
Your primary treatment medication (i.e. testosterone)
We offer payment plans every 4 weeks, 12 weeks, or 48 weeks. Longer plans mean bigger payments each time, but they save you money overall compared to the 4-week plan. Regardless of which plan you choose, the medication shipping schedule remains the same. Please let us know if you have any questionsâ€”weâ€™re happy to help.
Frequently Asked Questions (FAQs)
Can I get an invoice sent to me to pay for it instead of being automatically charged?
Billing: Can you send me an invoice?
We operate on a subscription system, so we do not send payment invoices. Once the charge is run it will recur based on the billing cycle.
Can you split the payments up?
Billing: No Split Payments
We cannot split payments up due to the way our subscription system works.
Do you accept Paypal?
Billing: No PayPal
We do not take Paypal at this time. You need to use a debit/credit card for payments.
Can you take money orders?
Billing: No Money Orders
We cannot process money orders at this time.
Do you guys accept CareCredit payment methods?
Billing: No CareCredit
You can certainly try to use CareCredit, but we are not aware of any patients in our care being able to use it successfully. 
Do you guys accept FSA/HSA payment methods?
Billing: FSA/HSA cards
We are happy to provide any information that may help with FSA/HSA reimbursement. Many of our patients have successfully received their fees reimbursed, but we cannot guarantee that FSA/HSA will cover our services. Please let us know if that works for you.
I want to update my payment method.
Billing: Changing Payment Method
Hello - You can change your payment method using this link:
Please note that the link will expire within 48 hours for security reasons.
When will my next refill date/charge date be?
Billing: Next Charge/Refill Date
Your next charge or refill date is MM/DD/YYYY. Once the charge is processed successfully, the pharmacy will begin processing your order. Your order should be shipped out within 2-5 business days from the charge date. You will receive a tracking link as soon as the order is shipped.
Do you guys accept insurance?
Billing: Insurance
Fountain does not participate with insurance. We certainly understand the reasons to try to find an insurance-based program. However, it doesn't work for Fountain's model for a few reasons. T therapy is frequently not well covered or covered only after a large deductible is paid each year. Treatment decisions are often directed by insurance companies and instead of the shared decisions of patients and providers. Fountain's billing model is based on an all-inclusive fee that recurs periodically and covers all aspects of service (medication, supplies, shipping costs, taxes, provider visits, labs, and 24/7 direct access to providers and support team via our text messages platform). We are able to give more personalized attention and time to fewer individual patient.
Do you offer any discounts?
Promotion: No Discount
We do not currently offer any discounts.
Do you offer a veteranâ€™s discount?
Promotion: Veteran's Discount
Hi - we appreciate your service, but at this time we do not offer a Veteran's discount.
Can I get a receipt for my most recent subscription charge?
Billing: Receipt
No problem. We will send you a receipt from our payment processor, Stripe. It will be sent to the email we have on file. 
Can I prepay my next charge?
Billing: Can I prepay my next charge?
Due to how our backend is structured, we're unable to process prepayment or split payments up.
Can I use Afterpay?
Billing: Do you accept Afterpay?
We do not accept Afterpay. You need to use a debit/credit card for payments.
Can I use my bank account for payments?
Billing: Can I use my bank account for payments?
At this time, we're not able to link your bank account to our payment processor. You will need to use a debit/credit card for payments.
What payment processor do you use?
Billing: What service do you use for payments?
Payments are processed through Stripe.
Can the charges be billed weekly or are all the costs upfront?
Billing: Can charges be billed weekly?
All plans are billed upfront and recur on a subscription cycle.
Is there a contract?
General CS: Is there a contract?
We do not have a contract. You can reach out at any point before your next scheduled subscription charge to cancel the service.
How do I let you know if I want to cancel my subscription?
Refunds: How to Cancel
You can cancel at any time by texting us before your next billing cycle. There is no contract. You would just need to text before your next charge is run.
How much notice do you need before canceling my subscription?
Refunds: How much notice do you need if I want to cancel?
You would simply need to let us know before your next charge is run. We can process a refund if your card is charged, but no order has gone out. If the order has been processed, we would be unable to process a refund.
Can you use Medi-Cal?
Billing: Can you accept Medi-Cal?
We're not able to accept medi-cal at this time.
I don't want recurring charges. Is there a way around this?
Billing: I don't want recurring charges
Due to the way our backend system is set up you will need to be on a subscription plan. That being said, you can cancel at any time before your next charge date - there is no contract.
Billing: Recurring Charge
All plans recur on a billing cycle and are paid for upfront. It looks like this was indicated when you set up your plan. Of course, you can cancel or modify your plan at any time you want. Please let us know how we can help. Thanks so much!
â€‹
Do you offer price-matching?
Promotion: No Price-Matching
We do not offer price-matching.
I'd like to be notified before my subscription renews.
Billing: I'd like to be notified before my next subscription charge is run
Your next charge date is: It is billed every: . Please note that this billing cycle may be adjusted if there are failed payments due to the way our backend is set up. 
At this time we're not able to provide you with a notification before the subscription renewal. We're actively working on a patient portal that will make this information more visible. We'll notify you once it's set up.
Can you send me an invoice?
Billing: Can you send me an invoice?
We operate on a subscription system, so we do not send payment invoices. Once the charge is run it will recur based on the billing cycle.
Can you just bill me for the medication, not the other services?
Billing: Can you just bill me for the medication, not the other services?
Fountain patients pay a subscription cost that bundles in multiple different services (i.e support, consultation, shipping, medication.) We do not charge you specifically for the medication alone due to the way our backend is structured.

Employee Pricing
Employee Pricing
Overview:
Fountain is now offering employee pricing! The system for handling employee pricing differs from the standard subscription system. For the time being, shift supervisors should set up pricing for employees.
Immediate Family- This is also eligable to Employee spouses/kids/parents
Pricing:
Employee pricing is based on this spreadsheet. Please take a look at the tab 'Employee Pricing.'
â€‹
The document outlines the regular pricing versus the team pricing we offer to Fountain Employees.
We offer employee discounts for all plans other than topical dutasteride, Spironolactone, Tirzepatideide Injections, and Anastrozole.
Setup:
Since we are not able to send the checkout links through Intercom, we would need to set up the plans for employees manually.
Here are the steps we should take to get this set up properly
1) Confirm what plans the employee wants (they may wish to add-on plans in addition to the main plan.)
2) Once this has been confirmed, we need to set up the subscription manually. The easiest way to do this is to search for the plan name (i.e., TRT plan) and add the value charged (i.e., 100). You can also put in the Stripe product ID listed on the spreadsheet, and you should see the product populate in Stripe.
3) Once the subscription (or subscriptions if there are add-ons needed) is set, we should let the employee know.
4) We need to create a billing ticket for the patient manually. In the billing ticket, we should specify the plans for which they were charged and assign the ticket to the MAs (defaulting to the billing team).

Medication Mgt
Syringes Workflow
Syringes Workflow
Overview
Patients on injections typically inject twice a week, with some deviations for different treatment plans.
Most T cyp orders are filled by Curexa or The Pharmacy Hub (TPH.)
Curexa sends 40 syringes in each order.
Patients on injections receive needles as a bundled item with their package. The package includes the injection vial, the needles, and alcohol prep pads.
The patient only needs to purchase a sharps needle case on their own.
TPH sends out 30 syringes in each order.
Patients on injections receive needles as a bundled item with their package. The package includes the injection vial, the needles, and alcohol prep pads.
The patient only needs to purchase a sharps needle case on their own.
If needed, they would be able to ship out needles without a testosterone RX (unlike Curexa.) We would prefer not to do this since the shipping cost would likely make it more expensive than filling the order at a local pharmacy.
Patients usually find the provided needles satisfactory, but occasionally, they request different types of syringes.
We cannot do this because our partner pharmacies bundle supplies together.
They are welcome to purchase them individually and receive account credit for the value of the package.
Our medical team can submit these prescriptions if patients have difficulty purchasing them locally. Patients sometimes request more needles because they break or lose them.
This is not an option unless there was an error in the original order.
However, we have two alternatives available for these patients.
Extra Needle Requests
Sometimes, patients run out of needles early. The most common reasons are that they break, lose them, or inject at a different cadence.
If a patient indicates that they ran out of needles or are on track to, please let them know when their next order will be processed. The next refill date will likely coincide with their request.
If the patient received fewer syringes than they should have from Curexa.
Curexa: Clarify why patient ran out of needles
Hello - shipments of T cypionate are sent every 10 weeks and should include 40 syringes. Any idea why you may have run out early? Do you think the pharmacy shipped fewer than 40 syringes?
If the patient received fewer syringes than they should have from TPH
TPH: Missing Needles
Hello - shipments from TPH should contain 30 needles. Do you think the pharmacy filled the order incorrectly? If so, we'll contact them and get that addressed.
If you confirm the patient received fewer syringes, send the appropriate snippet to the pharmacy:
If they received fewer needles than they should from Curexa or Belmar
Pharmacy sent fewer syringes than they should have
Hello,
The patient who received order # indicated that they received fewer syringes than they should have in the package. Is it possible to process a new order of syringes for them? Thank you!
Once we email our partner pharmacy and receive confirmation that they can send the needles out
Needles - after emailing pharmacy
Our partner pharmacy confirmed that they will process an order with the missing needles. We will update you as soon as we can. Hope you have a great day.
Other Reason
Situation
Macro
Communication
If a patient indicates they are unable to purchase the syringes
TRT MED: EasyTouch insulin syringes injections amazon link
NOTE: Amazon isn't able to fill this order in all states.
These syringes are very similar to the ones our partner pharmacy ships with medication. These have 0.5 mL of capacity while our partner pharmcy ships 1 mL vials. Otherwise theyâ€™re exactly the same.

Frequently Asked Questions (FAQs)
Question
Macro
Communication
Can I pick up the needles at a local pharmacy?
Pharmacy: Can I pick up the needles at a local pharmacy?
You may be able to pick up the needles at a local pharmacy, but we can't guarantee that they will be available.
What kind of needles are prescribed?
Medical: What kind of needles are prescribed?
The needles are: 27G 1/2" 1 cc syringes.
What should I do if I need more needles for my treatment?
Orders: Request for Additional Needles
Due to the way our partner pharmacies processes orders they wouldn't be able to send you more needles - unless a package was filled incorrectly. We're working on ways to make this more seamless - but in the meantime we do have two other options available.
You may be able to purchase the needles our partner pharmacy sends out off amazon. Here's the link: https://www.amazon.com/EasyTouch-Insulin-Syringe-Needle-2-Inch/dp/B081VV1PN3?crid=3JSBNF4TPCUCV&keywords=insulin+syringe+ez+27&qid=1697226980&s=hpc&sprefix=insulin+syringe+ez+27%2Chpc%2C65&sr=1-1
Please note that they are not able to process these orders in every state. If you encounter an issue purchasing the syringes please let us know, and we can ask our medical team to submit an order for syringes to a local pharmacy. Please just let us know the pharmacy name and phone number, and we'll get right on that.
Please note we can add an equal credit to your account if you use either option which would apply to your next subscription charge.

General Tech Issues Workflow
General Tech Issues Workflow
Overview
Some potential patients who attempt to register are blocked by our system. Our goal is to move them through the process and to address the issues to the best of our ability.
This document covers essential troubleshooting tips that may resolve their problems. This is not comprehensive since each user has a unique configuration - and we cannot guarantee that we can fix every potential issue.
Most Common Errors
Error 400
When a patient encounters this error, please send the Tech: Error 400 macro:
Hi there, this error can be produced for 2 reasons:
If your address is greater than 35 characters long
If you are using a debit card or a credit card that is being blocked. If your address isn't particularly long, we recommend trying a different card. You'll be able to update this with us in the future.
Unable to start trial
image.png
This error typically occurs when patients attempt to register, but there is an issue with their payment method.
Please use the following macros in assisting the patient:
Tech: Unable to Start Trial
Check if using another payment method will resolve the issue.
Hi - can you please try using a different payment method? That may resolve this issue. Thank you.
2. Tech: Clear Cache on Desktop Browser
If the patient is using a desktop, please have them try to clear their cache first and try again.
Hi there!
We're sorry to hear that you're experiencing difficulties signing up on our website. Sometimes, issues like this can be resolved by clearing your browser's cache and cookies. Follow the steps below based on the device and browser you're using:
On Desktop/Laptop:
â€‹
â€‹Google Chrome:
Click on the three-dot menu icon at the top-right corner.
Select "Settings."
Scroll down and click on "Privacy and security."
Click on "Clear browsing data."
Make sure "Cookies and other site data" and "Cached images and files" are selected.
Choose "All time" as the time range.
Click on "Clear data."
Mozilla Firefox:
Click on the three-line menu icon at the top-right corner.
Select "Options" (or "Preferences" on macOS).
Click on "Privacy & Security" in the left sidebar.
Scroll down to the "Cookies and Site Data" section and click on "Clear Data."
Ensure "Cookies and Site Data" and "Cached Web Content" are selected.
Click on "Clear."
Safari:
Click on "Safari" in the top menu.
Select "Preferences."
Click on the "Privacy" tab.
Click on "Manage Website Data."
Click on "Remove All."
Confirm by clicking "Remove Now."
Microsoft Edge:
Click on the three-dot menu icon at the top-right corner.
Select "Settings."
Click on "Privacy, search, and services" in the left sidebar.
Under "Clear browsing data," click on "Choose what to clear."
Ensure "Cookies and other site data" and "Cached images and files" are selected.
Click on "Clear now."
â€‹
â€‹After clearing your cache and cookies, please try signing up on our website again. If you're still experiencing issues, don't hesitate to reach out to us for further assistance. We're here to help!
3. Tech: Clear Cache on Mobile Device
If the patient is using a mobile browser, please have them try to clear their cache first and try again.
On iOS Devices (iPhone/iPad):
Safari:
Open the "Settings" app.
Scroll down and tap on "Safari."
Scroll down and tap on "Clear History and Website Data."
Confirm by tapping "Clear History and Data."
On Android Devices (Chrome Browser):
Open the Chrome app.
Tap on the three-dot menu icon at the top-right corner.
Select "Settings."
Tap on "Privacy and Security."
Tap on "Clear browsing data."
Ensure "Cookies and site data" and "Cached images and files" are selected.
Choose the time range or select "All time."
Tap on "Clear data."
4. Tech: Incognito Mode
If they still have this issue, please have them try switching to Incognito browser.
Got it - can you please try registering using incognito mode? Incognito mode is typically accessed by either using the keyboard combination control-shift-n.
Another way to do this is to click on the three vertical dots towards the top right of your browser window and select 'new incognito window.
5. Tech: Patient can't register in a state we operate
If the basic troubleshooting for browsers do not work, please send the patient the following message:
We operate in your state. Can you please try using another zip code to register? We can change your information in our system after you register.
Sorry, Fountain is not available in your area.
Please ask the patient which state they are located in and check to see if they are in a state where we are operating. Please use this map as a reference: Fountain Active States
If they are in a state where we do not operate
General CS: If we don't operate in the state a patient is attempting to register in
Got it. At this time, we do not operate in your state. We hope to expand there soon!
If they are in a state where we operate
Tech: Patient can't register in a state we operate
We operate in your state. Can you please try using another zip code to register? We can change your information in our system after you register.
â€‹
No 2FA code
Our system requires patients to put in an authentication code when they register. This is done for security purposes. We cannot provide them with this code ourselves.
If a lead indicates they have issues registering and they have not registered before, please send them the macro: 2FA Code Issue
If they have already sent over a screenshot, you can remove the part about the screenshot.
Once they provide the phone number and email they are using to attempt to register, please send a message to the tech-cs channel and tag Rob. We will work on addressing the issue.
If a patient indicates they have this problem, please send the General CS: Message to team macro to the patient:
Ok thanks for sending this over. I'd need to get this over to someone on our team who can better address this for you. You'll hear back from us as soon as possible.
Take Note: Please contact Rob via tech-cs channel and indicate that the unregistered lead encountered the issue. Please provide any additional information which may be of use.
Greyed out Labs
If a patient is saying the lab scheduling link shows all options as greyed out we need to verify that it is not just a technical issue.
To do this, please do the following:
-Have the patient try again with the same link. They can do this by hitting the back button, enter the address again, and see if options populate.
-If options still do not populate, CS should check if there are any nearby locations via Quest and Labcorp.
>If there are valid locations with either facility near the patient, please send the Intercom or Akute link via the Tech-CS Intercom Channel for tech to look into.
>If no locations are found by the CS team member, proceed with the greyed out lab macro as this indicates there is no tech error.
Issues with Video Visits
Some patients have issues connecting through Doxy, Google Meet, or other platforms.
This issue transpires for one of three reasons:
The permissions on their web browser/app are not enabled.
They have a broken microphone/camera
They simply need to restart their device due to updates/drivers/etc.
If we receive a ticket from the medical team indicating we need to assist the patient with connection/device the best option is typically to send them an instant google meet link.
You can send them the macro: Video Visit Connection Issues.
Once they confirm, you can simply click on this link: https://meet.google.com/landing, select 'New Meeting,' and send it to the patient via text.
Once you get on the call with the patient, please test basic settings with them (i.e make sure they can hear us, you can hear them, and that their webcam is working.)
You do not need to turn on your webcam for this. We just need to make sure they can hear us, and we can see them.
We will not realistically be able to resolve all issues for them if they have a dead microphone or camera or have device issues outside the scope of this workflow since we cannot remotely access their device. That being said, we should assist them to the best of our ability.
Patients with Intermingled Information
Occasionally patients have intermingled information due to the way they signed up.
One of the following likely occurred:
They signed up on behalf of someone else with some of their own information (i.e phone number, email.)
They were trying to register for TRT and HRT programs through the same account. In their minds, the TRT account would belong to one person, while the HRT account would belong to another.
Patients cannot have intermingled information since we need separate profiles. We cannot write RXes for patients with the wrong information, and it presents a HIPAA issue if we provide personal information to the wrong party.
If this situation comes up we need to do the following:
Send them the macro: Tech Issue: Interlinked Profiles
Your profile may have an issue due to how the evaluation was completed. It looks like you may have information (e.g., an email, and a phone number) that is attached to someone else. However, we'd be happy to help address this problem. We just need you to confirm your first name, last name, active email, and phone number. Once we do that, they can register right away without any issues. Thanks so much!
Once they confirm, we will update their information on Akute. If they know someone interested in the program, they must register using the evaluation links.
We shouldn't create accounts for them without going through the evaluation. Although we technically can create Akute and Stripe accounts for them independently of the assessment, a multitude of technical issues will arise.
Messages Not Going Through
If a text message fails to go through, there are typically two reasons for this:
1) They have blocked our number. This may have been done unintentionally. We should email them (not text them), the macro:
Undelivered Messages: Text Start
Hello from Fountain! We sent you a text earlier from 833-967-3100, but it looks like our message did not go through. Is it possible that you blocked our number? If so, please text us at 833-967-3100 with the message: 'START.' Thank you!
2) They currently do not have service, so the message has not gone through. We should temporarily email them (if needed.)
Evaluation Links Not Working
-If a lead indicates the evaluation links are not opening up on their end, we need to resend them the link to the evaluation. Please make sure you send them the correct evaluation link (TRT or HRT.)
-While we aren't sure why the links aren't opening up on their end (it may be due to their browser, extensions, or internet connection), the links we sent out are working.
Other Tech Issues
If a patient indicates another issue not covered in this document, please get in touch with Rob via the tech-cs channel. These issues may include, but are not limited to, the following:
The patient's Intercom profile is not syncing with Akute
Displayed info for orders not being accurate on Intercom
An API call did not go out as intended.
â€‹

The Pharmacy Hub
The Pharmacy Hub Workflow
The Pharmacy Hub Workflow
Overview
We can contact their support team for all order concerns and inquiries at support@thepharmacyhub.com
The Pharmacy Hubâ€™s manufacturer is BPI Labs/Blue Vase
We do not currently have an API system with The Pharmacy Hub. We should within the coming months.
As soon as prescriptions are written, they process the order.
They ship all medications to all states, like Curexa.
Refills are processed by medical assistants handling pharmacy tasks.
Medications Prescribed
The Pharmacy Hub is currently processing semaglutide for GLP patients.
Providers must send a separate prescription for the syringes.
They process all Enclomphine orders for TRT patients, and the vast majority of T cypionate orders.
Shipping and Addresses
The Pharmacy Hub sends all orders via UPS or Fedex. There is no signature requirement.
TPH typically processes and ships out orders within the day.
Patients should receive tracking links over text and email from our number once the order goes out.
If we need to update a patient's address, please email TPH. They will need this to send future orders to that location.
TPH sends orders to the patient's home address by default. TPH can send orders to UPS access points if a patient does not want to receive shipments at home. However, if we change a patient's address to a UPS access point, TPH will need to be notified via email since they don't typically ship there without an explicit request.
Since they ship orders via UPS, they cannot deliver to a Walgreens/FedEx site.
Order Statuses
-Patient's order status should be reflected on the sidebar on Intercom.
-You can see if an order is in progress, shipped, or needs clarification.
Searching For Orders (MAs Only)
After logging in, go to Pharmacy and select Workflow. This will display all orders under our account.
If you need to search for a specific patient's order, you can look them up using this format (Last Name, First Name)
You can also view orders by selecting â€œYâ€ in the Print filter, which will display orders with tracking numbers.
Export CSV file for Orders (MAs Only)
On the sidebar menu, click Reports and select Batch.
Select Pharmacy Customer Order Summary and enter a start and end date to view orders in the report. You can also leave the fields blank to display today's orders.
Click Submit.
The report entry will be displayed below, with a CSV highlight button that will allow you to download the file.
After downloading the file, you'll find details such as Order ID, Tracking Number and Status, Patient Name and DOB, Medication, and more. You can use this sheet to send tracking links to patients instead of navigating through the portal list.
Please note that a single patient may have multiple entries for syringes, vials, and alcohol prep pads, but they should all share the same tracking number.
Sending Tracking Information (MAs Only)
To find the tracking number, click on the order ID to view the order details.
This will display the various stages of order processing, including the UPS tracking number.
Copy the tracking number, open a new browser tab, and go to Google. Then, paste the tracking number into the search bar.
Copy the UPS link or URL.
For GLP patients: Please use the macro: GLP: TPH Tracking Link and add the tracking link to the macro.
For TRT patients: Please use the macro: TRT/HRT Tracking Links and add the tracking link to the macro.
Processing Refills (MAs Only)
Since we do not have API integration with TPH, refills for patients are processed based on a retool page. This is handled by our MAs.
Steps to Request a Refill from TPH:
Access the Dispense Pro Portal
Log in to the Dispense Pro portal: Dispense Pro - The Pharmacy Hub
Click on Pharmacy in the side menu and select Workflow.
Locate the Patientâ€™s Last Order
Search for the patientâ€™s name.
Open their last order by clicking the Order ID.
Identify the Rx Number
Review the order processing stages displayed.
Select the PV1 stage to find the Rx Number associated with the order.
Note: GLP patients will have two Rx numbers (one for the vial and another for the syringes)
Send an Email to TPH: support@thepharmacyhub.com
Include the following details:
Patientâ€™s full name.
Rx Number
They will respond to the email to confirm that orders have been generated/processed.
Order Concerns and Cancellation Requests
For order concerns, such as being shipped with improper supplies or being sent to the wrong address, we can email support@thepharmacyhub.com
Since you won't have access to the order ID or order number itself, please provide the patient's name and DOB.
Early Refills
-Please contact TPH via their support email if a patient needs an early refill due to a broken vial or a package lost by the carrier.
-If the vial was broken, we need to ask the patient to send a screenshot of the vial for processing purposes. That should be included in the email we send to TPH for authorization.
-If the carrier lost an order, please provide the tracking link for the order to TPH and indicate that the airline lost it. The tracking link should indicate either a 'delay', 'lost package', or simply not update for several days.
-If the patient needs an early refill due to a dosage change, please indicate that in the early refill request to TPH. They may ask for the provider's contact information to confirm dosing/other information.
Frequently Asked Questions (FAQs)
QUESTION
MACRO
COMMUNICATION
Can you send me tracking details for my order?
GLP: TPH Tracking Link
Hi,
Here's the tracking link for your GLP medication: (LINK)
Please closely monitor your tracking link to ensure you receive it as soon as it's delivered.
Your medication is stable for 60 days below 94Â°F and 5 days below 104Â°F. Please refrigerate it upon arrival.
My order is delayed, and Iâ€™m worried the medication may not be effective when it arrives.
TPH: GLP Temperature Guidelines
Our partner pharmacy has conducted stability and temperature excursion studies to ensure the medication can be shipped in temperature conditions up to 104Â°F for up to 5 days. If you experience any issues with the package upon receipt, please inform us immediately.

General lab info
Third Party Labs
Third Party Labs
Overview
We partner with Labcorp, Getlabs, and Quest Diagnostics for lab testing.
We can cover lab fees in most states for patients using these services without any issues.
If an established patient does not have one of our lab partners as an available option third party testing may be considered.
Process
Most hospitals and testing facilities can perform the tests our providers order for patients.
If an established patient does not have any Labcorp/Quest location options populate when utilizing our lab scheduling link we may consider third party labs. Please verify there are no lab partners near them.
We may also confirm if the patient is possibly traveling to an area with lab partners in the near future. If they are traveling, we can try to find a Labcorp/Quest near them. This is a much more seamless process and allows us to cover the fees if patient books with one of our lab partners.
If this is an established patient that does not have a lab partner available to them, and they are not traveling in the near future please do the following:
Send a ticket to the shift supervisor team for review/approval.
If appropriate, the Shift Supervisor will then send a ticket to the team lead provider for the third party lab order.
Lab order can then be sent directly to the patient.
We should request the patient send us the results once available. The results will not automatically sync with our system.
We must ask our medical team to submit a new lab order for these patients. The default lab order has no signature, so it cannot be used at a third-party lab location.
A signature is not needed for Quest/Labcorp since it's sent electronically - but any third-party lab location will need a signature.
Home Test Kits
We are working with Tasso, which can send home test kits to these patients regardless of location.
This would be built into the standard evaluation process. Patients just need to pay the fee, and they can be sent over.
We do not have a firm date as to when this will be set up - and as such should not be mentioned to patients.
â€‹
â€‹
â€‹

Trustpilot Workflow
Trustpilot Workflow
Overview
Trustpilot is an online review community that's open to everyone to use - customers, businesses and the community.
If a patient provides positive feedback, we want to capitalize on that!
Most patients are happy with our serviceâ€”we're currently the highest-rated medical company in the US. That being said, we can always use more reviews!
Appropriateness
We don't want to ask for reviews simply because someone says: "thank you", or a generic positive affirmation. We want to highlight effusive patients praising us and our service.
When we receive these types of messages, send the appropriate review snippet based on the patient's program:
TRT: Trustpilot Link
We are happy to help. If you'd be interested, we'd really appreciate a review from you.
Here's the link: https://www.trustpilot.com/review/fountaintrt.com
You don't have to use your real name if you would prefer not to. Have a great day!
HRT: Trustpilot Link
We are happy to help. If you'd be interested, we'd really appreciate a review from you.
Here's the link: https://www.trustpilot.com/review/fountain.net
You don't have to use your real name if you would prefer not to. Have a great day!
GLP: Trustpilot Link
We are happy to help. If you'd be interested, we'd really appreciate a review from you.
Here's the link: https://www.trustpilot.com/review/fountainglp.com
You don't have to use your real name if you would prefer not to. Have a great day!
We should add Trustpilot and the date we requested the review to Intercom User notes. For example,
Username
What can I choose as my display name in the review?
Users are free to select a username of their choice. Many of our patients keep it simple by using only their first name, especially if they're concerned about privacy, like not wanting others to know their medical history. Others are comfortable displaying their full name.
For those who prefer to remain anonymous, they can choose a different username, such as Honest Reviewer.
If a patient needs help changing their username, please refer them to the TrustPilot Help Center for instructions and a video guide.
Frequently Asked Questions (FAQs)
I canâ€™t figure out how to only use my first name. I donâ€™t want other people to know my medical history.
Trustpilot: Anonymous
We totally understand. TrustPilot recommends using a different name than your actual name in order to leave an anonymous review (example: â€œHonest Reviewerâ€).
How can I find reviews?
For Trustpilot: Find TRT Reviews
â€‹
You can find reviews by checking us out on our Trustpilot page here: https://www.trustpilot.com/review/fountaintrt.com
For Trustpilot: Find HRT Reviews
â€‹
â€‹You can find reviews by checking us out on our Trustpilot page here: https://www.trustpilot.com/review/fountain.net
For Trustpilot: Find GLP Reviews
â€‹
â€‹You can find reviews by checking us out on our Trustpilot page here: https://www.trustpilot.com/review/fountainglp.com
Will I get a discount if I give a review?
Promotion: Discount for a review
We certainly appreciate your support, but we cannot offer any promotions or discounts for reviews.

Shipping and Logistics
When a Patient Moves or is Traveling
When a Patient Moves or is Traveling
Overview
This workflow outlines how to manage patients who either relocate to a new state or are temporarily traveling. We need to make sure patient records remain accurate, prescriptions are managed smoothly, and patients are fully informed of their options during their move or travel.
Permanent Relocation to a New State
This process applies only if a patient moves to a different state. If the patient moves within the same state, skip steps involving state updates.
Update Patient Address in Akute.
The new address will automatically sync with Intercom, but state tags on Intercom must be updated manually.â€‹
Update State Tags in Intercom.
Locate patient's Lead Tags section.
Remove the old state tag by clicking or the X icon or Remove.
Add the new state tag by clicking the + icon or Add tag
Type and select the patient's new state
Update address with pharmacy (if necessary)
If the patient is prescribed medication through TPH or Belmar pharmacy, we need to contact the pharmacy and ask them to update the patient's address. The address will not default to the address currently in our system.
If the patient is prescribed medication through Curexa there is no need to do anything. Please just make sure you update the address in our system and the patient tag.
â€‹
Program Availability in the New State
Check if we operate in the patient's new state by referring to: Fountain Active States Map
If we operate in the state
Send the below macro then follow the permanent relocation steps:
General CS: If a patient moves to a state where we operate
Great! We operate in that state. We'll update your address and ask our partner pharmacy to send future orders out there. Have a great day.
If we do not operate in the state
Send the below macro:
General CS: Moving to a state where we do not operate
Got it - thanks for letting us know. Unfortunately, we don't operate in the state you're moving to. Will this be your permanent address moving forward? If so, we would need to cancel your subscription.
If the patient confirms this is their permanent address
Send the below macro then follow the proper cancellation flow:
Cancel Subscription: Moved to state we do not operate in
Understood. We'll need to cancel your membership since we do not operate in your state. Wishing you the best.
Patients Temporarily Traveling
Patients can schedule labs at LabCorp or Quest during travel.
Inform them that their visit must be scheduled in a licensed state once results are available.
Medication Shipments
Confirm the patient's temporary address using the macro:
Orders: Confirm patient's current address
We can ask our partner pharmacy to see if they can ship there. Can you confirm the current address you are staying at?
Contact the pharmacy to confirm shipping feasibility.
Pharmacy: Sending patient's temporary address
Hello, Would you be able to ship to this address:
â€‹A patient in our care is temporarily staying there. Thank you!
If the pharmacy can ship to the travel location
Temporarily update the address in Akute. Use the Sticky Notes feature in Akute to store the patient's permanent address while temporarily updating the demographics section with the travel address for patients who are temporarily traveling.
Process a refill and create a ticket to revert the address after the patient returns. Depending on the duration of their travel we may need to adjust their refill and/or charge date.
If shipping is not possible
Please send the below macro to the patient:
Pharmacy: Unable to ship to address
We reached out to our partner pharmacy, and they indicated that they are unable to ship to:
at this time.

Discount Codes and Communication
Discount Codes and Communication
Overview:
Some patients indicate they received discount codes from one of our partners, or are confusing us with someone else. We cannot honor discount codes from a third party that is not affilated with us, and we need to make that clear to them.
1. Initial Assessment
When a patient mentions a discount code please ask Ask the patient:
â€œCan you tell us what discount code you received and where you saw it? Can you provide a link to the website you saw if you recall please?â€
2. Determine the Likely Scenario
There are three possible explanations:
Scenario 1: Mistaken for Another Service
Description: The patient is confusing Fountain with another service that does offer discount codes.
Action: Politely explain that we do not offer discount codes for membership and they may be referencing a different provider.
Scenario 2: Referring to Discounted Assessment Fee
Description: The patient is referring to a discounted assessment link provided by a partner or affiliate.
Action: Explain:
â€œWhile we do not offer discount codes, some of our partners and affiliates provide a special link to reduce the $35 assessment fee (e.g., to $30 or less). This might be what you saw.â€
Scenario 3: Misunderstanding a Partner Link as a Code
Description: A partner may promote a discounted assessment link, and the patient misinterprets this as a promo code.
Action: Clarify that the discount is applied through the link itself, not via a promo code entry.
4. Internal Notes
Please don't promise or suggest that discount codes are available in the future.
Use macros where available to respond efficiently.
If unsure, escalate to the 'Affiliates/Third Parties' team on Intercom.

SmartRecover Process
Overview:

-They text patients through other numbers:
For TRT: 573-595-6044

Internal CS Processes
After Hours Policy
After Hours Policy
Patient Communication After Hours and Holidays
General Communication
Please refrain from proactively texting/calling the patients after 9pm, unless it is evident that the patient is awake.
We can determine if the patient is awake if they have recently interacted with a member of our team/a ticket has been created for them by the medical team.
If patients send us outgoing messages, it is important that we respond, as they are clearly still awake and will not be irritated.
Regardless of the patient's time zone or state, if they are clearly still awake, we can assume that they will not be bothered by a message (provided we respond promptly).
Graveyard Shift
Team members working the graveyard shift should address any inbound messages. The patient is expecting a response if they reach out.
Take any calls, regardless of the time. They are already interested in speaking with us.
Work on any projects/routine tasks (i.e Retool Audits, Itemized Receipts.).
Third-Party Communication During the Holidays
We should still communicate with all of our third-party partners to the best of our ability during the holidays.
They may not be working/be available, but that doesn't make a difference. They will get back to us as soon as they can.
Please note that the exception to this is Quest/Labcorp. We may not be able to contact them to check on patients' results during the holidays since they'll be closed. We will need to contact them after the holidays.
Internal Team Communication During the Holidays
Most team members will be off - but we will always have at least one person online during all hours. Use all the regular channels you would typically use for team communication regardless of the holiday.
The Medical Team and the Holidays
Depending on the holiday, some providers may work, but most will not. We can still assign tickets to them as needed, and they will address when they can.
The RNs and SS teams may be working limited hours during the holidays. We should still create tickets for the RNs as needed.
Please make sure you mark any tickets regarding side effects as a priority for the RNs so they can address them as soon as possible.
We can reach out to patients based on the recommendation of the RNs/providers since these may be medical issues.
If a patient is having side effects or a medical issue and is anxious or upset, please use the critical escalations WhatsApp group.
Weekends
Team members should treat the weekend like a weekday.
We can and should contact patients who are due for labs, despite it being a weekend. Labcorp has limited availability on the weekends, but we can still schedule patients for the coming week.
We should assign tickets to providers on the weekend, regardless of whether they're working or not. Some members of the medical team do work on the weekends. If they do not, they'll simply check the task the next time they are working.
For any tickets regarding side effects that need to be addressed by the medical team, please remember to start the ticket or mark it as a priority.
Tickets
Any tickets for follow ups on lab scheduling/failed payments should be snoozed till the next day.
The only ticket that we should initiate proactive contact for would be the 'Schedule Getlabs Appointment', ticket type. These patients are expecting us to schedule them. Unlike Quest/Labcorp these patients must be scheduled manually by a member of our team.

Platform Guides
Akute Workflow
Akute Workflow
Overview
AkuteHealth is an Electronic Medical Record (EMR) system. It offers solutions for providers and CS team to effectively manage patient information, medical notes/ forms/ documents, lab orders, and more. Additionally, it integrates with Retool for order management and Stripe for billing purposes.
AkuteHealth
Akute Main Menu
The left-side Main Menu in Akute is available for the following purposes:
Inbox - To find the list of all tasks (i.e. for lab order, refill script, etc.) and faxed lab results
Patient List - To search for a patient and manually add a patient if they are not signed up by the system automatically
Patient - To check a specific patientâ€™s details, preferences, progress notes, medications, labs and other documents
Patient Sidebar
When you pull up a patient's profile, you will have access to the following features for different purposes:
Tasks - To view or create tasks, such as lab orders or refill scripts.
Medications - To view the current medications of the patient.
Labs - To check lab results and quickly access the Healthgorilla page.
Notes - To review progress notes entered by the provider.
Documents - To view or manually add documents, such as lab results.
Demographics - To view and edit the patient's details, such as address, date of birth, phone number, and email address.
Forms - To view updates on patient's treatment.
Orders - To view Lab orders.
Faxes - To view or resend the documents that was previously sent to the patient.
Tasks
Akute Tasks - Quest Orders (TRT)
Click on Add Task
Enter the following details:
Task: Quest
Tags:
For New Signups:
â€˜new labsâ€™
â€˜Quest - provider must includeâ€™
For Monitoring Labs:
â€˜f/u labsâ€™
â€˜Quest - provider must include diagnosis codesâ€™
Priority: Urgent
Due Date: Current date
Task Owner: Labs
Click on Save
Akute Tasks - Labcorp Orders (TRT)
Click on Add Task
Enter the following details:
Task: LC [appointment date]
Tags: new labs
Priority: Urgent
Due Date: Current date
Task Owner: Labs
Click on Save
Akute Medications
If you click View Pharmacy, a pop-window will appear, showing where the patient's medication is from.
Akute Labs
Patient lab results can be found in this section. Click Order Labs to quickly access the Healthgorilla page if you need to locate a missing lab order that does not appear on Akute.
This will take you to the landing page where you can create a new order. To go to the homepage, simply click on the HealthGorilla logo (gorilla icon).
Akute Notes and Sharing Medical Notes
If we need to know who was the provider the patient last saw, when the last video visit was, or when the routine labs (next lab visit) are due, we can review the Progress Notes in this section.
These are documents created by healthcare professionals to update a patient's medical records.
Sharing Medical Notes
Click the kebab (:) menu below the patient's name on Akute
Select Print Clinical Profile from the options
When the Chart Export screen appears, click Generate PDF to automatically export all the charts
Download and add the file in the Akute Documents
Send a secure message called 'Medical Notes,' and attach the medical notes to the message.
Documents and Faxes
-If we need to check for Faxes on Akute we can click on 'documents', on the sidebar.
Akute Forms (GLP)
GLP patients submit weekly forms that provide updates on their treatment.
The patients will receive an automated text on a weekly basis/ The text will contain a link to the form and once they submit the form, it will be uploaded to their Akute chart.
Akute Orders
This shows the list of lab orders created for the patient.
Facility: Where the lab tests will be done
Orders: Tests ordered for the patient
Date: When the lab order is created
Reference #:Lab Order ID or Requisition Number (6-digit order ID is for Labcorp/ Quest)
Akute Faxes and Faxing Documents
Faxes section shows you the fax history and allows you to view or resend the document that was previously sent over to the patient.
Akute Tasks - Labcorp Orders (TRT)
Go to Documents
Click the document to be faxed over for the patient
Click the kebab (:) menu button at the right side of the file name
Select Fax from the options
Complete the Fax Details
Click Generate
Instructional Video
Faxing without a patient chart:
-On the off-chance we receive a fax not intended for a patient in our care or our practice, we can fax over the document. This is an uncommon occurence, but the process for dealing with this is below.
â€‹
Upload the document to Inbox->Documents
Click into the document
Click the 3-dot icon at the top right
Select the fax option
You can then select a recipient and add a cover letter message. There is no additional customization of the outgoing fax
Akute Sticky Notes
Patients sometimes have temporary addresses that we update. We should add their prior address to Akute under Sticky Notes in case it needs to be reverted.
In this example, you can see that the patient prefers to use a Walgreens address for order shipment. A CS staff member added a Sticky Note to save the patient's home address.
Akute Patient Portal - Inviting Patients
Akute Health has a Patient Portal that displays labs, vitals, and notes in viewable sections. When a patient requests to see their lab results, we need to check the Labs section to confirm that the lab results have been successfully added to their profile.
If lab results are available on Akute, follow these steps to invite a patient to access their lab results through the Patient Portal.
This option might be greyed out if they were previously invited to the portal.
Click on the kebab (â‹®) menu button below the patient's name
Select Invite to Patient Portal
Send the Akute: Sharing Lab Results macro
We would be happy to share your lab results. You should receive an email from Akute Health with instructions to access the portal. If you don't see it, please check your spam folder.
Akute Patient Portal - Email and Overview
When we send a patient an invite to the Patient Portal, they will receive an email from Akute with instructions.
Patient Point of View of Akute
Akute Patient Portal - Troubleshooting
Occasionally, patients have issues accessing the Patient Portal. These include, but are not limited to:
The 'sharing labs' option is unavailable and greyed out.
The patient is encountering an error when attempting to sign in.
The patient is unable to reset their password.
The labs do not display in the patient's portal account.
When these issues come up, we will need to contact Akute. Please tag Paytyn in Akute's Slack channel. They will need to diagnose the problem.
Internal Troubleshooting
Sometimes patients have issues accessing the portal.
If we have already invited a patient to the portal and the option is greyed out:
Send the Akute: Invite to Akute's Portal macro
We invited you to Akute's portal earlier. Did you receive an email from them? The email header should have been: Fountain Wellness - Patient Portal Code.
If the patient indicates that they cannot find the app/locate the email but we've sent them an invite:
Send the Akute: Download Akute's Portal macro
It looks like you may have signed up for Akute previously. You can download it via iOS (https://apps.apple.com/us/app/akute-patient-portal/id1571802525) and Android (https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=com.akutehealth.patientportal&pli=1) and click â€œForgot password to get back in.
Merging Akute Profiles
Instructional Video on Merging Patient Charts
Akute's Web Portal
â€‹
-Akute has a web portal that patients can access here: https://portal.akutehealth.com/login
-Some patients may prefer to use the web portal as opposed to the physical app, since it means they do not have to download anything.
-If patients have app-related issues (i.e the app not loading on their phone, closing randomly), we should suggest they use Akute's web portal.
â€‹
Secure Messaging
By default, patients only receive lab results through the Akute app. We can share documents with them securely by using the Conversation feature. We would prefer to share patient records through Akute as opposed to a third party messaging app since it is more secure, and most patients should already have the app downloaded.
Go to Conversations on Akute
Click +New Conversation
Select Secure Message
Click on Attach File and the files from the patientâ€™s documents section will load
Select the documents that you want to send to the patient
Click on Continue
Click on Send

Escalation Protocol and Communication
Approved Escalation Language & Patient Messaging
Purpose: Provide consistent, empathetic, and de-escalating language that reassures patients while allowing internal teams to act without increasing legal, clinical, or reputational risk.
1. Universal Acknowledgement Language (Use First)
Use one of the following before explaining next steps:
â€œI understand why this is frustrating, and I appreciate you bringing it to our attention.â€
â€œThank you for your patience â€” Iâ€™m actively looking into this now.â€
â€œI want to make sure we get this resolved correctly for you.â€
2. Level 0 â€” Standard Concern Messaging
Shipping / Processing Delays
â€œIâ€™m checking the status of your order now and will follow up as soon as I have an update.â€
â€œWeâ€™re reaching out to the pharmacy directly to confirm where things stand.â€
Labs / Scheduling
â€œIâ€™ll review your lab status and make sure everything needed is in place.â€
â€œIf thereâ€™s anything holding this up, Iâ€™ll let you know right away.â€
Billing / Charges
â€œIâ€™m reviewing your billing history now to make sure everything aligns correctly.â€
â€œOnce I confirm the details, Iâ€™ll walk you through the next steps.â€
3. Level 1 â€” Elevated Concern Messaging
When Patient Is Frustrated or Reaching Out Repeatedly
â€œI hear your concern, and I donâ€™t want this to keep going back and forth for you.â€
â€œIâ€™m escalating this internally so we can get you a clear answer and resolution.â€
When Involving a Supervisor
â€œIâ€™m looping in a supervisor to make sure this is reviewed thoroughly.â€
â€œI want to make sure this is handled at the right level so we donâ€™t miss anything.â€
Expectation Setting
â€œIâ€™ll follow up with you as soon as I have confirmation.â€
â€œIf anything changes before then, Iâ€™ll reach out right away.â€
4. Level 2 â€” Medical, Compliance, or Financial Risk Messaging
Medical / Side Effects (Non-Critical)
â€œYour health is important to us, and I want to make sure this is reviewed by our medical team.â€
â€œIâ€™m going to have a member of our medical team look at this and advise on next steps.â€
Compliance / Video Visit / Refill Issues
â€œFor compliance reasons, we do need a quick video visit before we can move forward.â€
â€œI know thatâ€™s inconvenient, but itâ€™s required so we can safely continue treatment.â€
Billing / Refund Review
â€œIâ€™m having this reviewed to determine whether a refund or adjustment is appropriate.â€
â€œOnce that review is complete, Iâ€™ll update you immediately.â€
5. Level 3 â€” Critical Escalation Messaging (Use Exactly)
Legal or Formal Complaints
Use once, then stop normal conversation
â€œI understand your concern. Iâ€™m escalating this to our leadership team for review.â€
â€œA member of our leadership or medical team will follow up as appropriate.â€
(Do not debate, justify, or provide timelines beyond this.)
Serious Medical Symptoms
â€œYour symptoms are concerning, and I want to make sure this is addressed immediately.â€
â€œIâ€™m escalating this to our medical leadership right now so they can guide next steps.â€
If appropriate:
â€œIf your symptoms worsen or you feel unsafe, please seek emergency medical care.â€
6. De-Escalation Language (When Emotions Are High)
Use these to lower tension without admitting fault:
â€œThatâ€™s very frustrating to deal with â€” weâ€™ll work on getting clarity here.â€
â€œI want to make sure we approach this the right way and get you accurate information.â€
â€œLetâ€™s take a moment to make sure weâ€™re aligned on what needs to happen next.â€
7. Language to Avoid (All Escalations)
Do NOT use:
â€œApologies for the inconvenienceâ€
â€œThereâ€™s nothing we can doâ€
â€œThatâ€™s our policyâ€
â€œCalm downâ€
â€œUnfortunatelyâ€¦â€
Replace with:
â€œHereâ€™s what we can do nextâ€
â€œThis is how weâ€™ll move forwardâ€
â€œWeâ€™ll address this directlyâ€
8. Closing the Loop (Resolution Messaging)
When resolved or transferred:
â€œThanks again for your patience â€” this has now been addressed.â€
â€œHereâ€™s a summary of what we did and what you can expect next.â€
â€œIf you need anything further, weâ€™re here to help.â€
